### [1] [Fully Unsupervised Detection of Physical Contacts on Subsea Cables via State-of-Polarization Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01484)
*Agastya Raj,Alvaro Doval,Tian Tian,Steinar Bjørnstad,Marco Ruffini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无监督的Fast-Slow DSVDD检测器，用于海底光缆偏振状态监测，成功检测到所有5次确认的拖网渔船接触事件，并发现了额外经证实的电缆接触事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海底光缆的偏振状态监测对于及时发现电缆接触事件（如拖网渔船碰撞）至关重要，传统方法依赖有标签数据，而实际场景中标签获取困难，因此需要开发无监督的异常检测方法。

Method: 采用Fast-Slow DSVDD（深度支持向量数据描述）检测器，该方法是完全无监督的，无需事件标签进行训练，适用于连续状态监测场景。

Result: 在122,174条记录中，该方法将所有5次已确认的拖网渔船接触事件排在前13位，并发现了额外的经证实的电缆接触事件。

Conclusion: 无监督的Fast-Slow DSVDD检测器在海底光缆偏振状态监测任务中表现优异，能够在大量数据中准确识别罕见的接触事件，证明了其在实际部署场景中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: We present a fully unsupervised Fast-Slow DSVDD detector for continuous State-of-Polarization monitoring on a deployed subsea cable. Trained without event labels, it ranks all five confirmed trawler contacts within the top 13 of 122,174 recordings and surfaces additional corroborated cable-contact events.

</details>


### [2] [Enabling Real-Time AI in O-RAN: Deploying andMeasuring AI Inside a Near-RT RIC xApp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01583)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Sampath Jayarathna,Safdar Hussain Bouk,Fahmida Afrin,C. Nicolas Barati,Neda Moghim,Valentina Nanou,Muhammad Enayetur Rahman,Sachin Shetty*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在O-RAN架构的Near-RT RIC中成功嵌入轻量级AI模型，推理延迟在微秒级别，满足10ms近实时控制预算，并发布了可复现的测试平台工具RIC Workbench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在RAN优化领域已被广泛研究，但很少有工作在实际Near-RT RIC软件控制循环中直接演示和测量AI推理的嵌入执行。现有研究缺乏在真实测试平台上验证AI模型能否满足Near-RT RIC严格时序约束（10ms到1s）的实证数据。

Method: 在OpenAirInterface和FlexRIC测试平台上实现AI使能的网络状态分类xApp；使用结构化合成数据集训练模型，数据集包含MAC、RLC、PDCP、GTP和UE计数等跨层RAN状态特征；采用逻辑回归和浅层多层感知器（MLP）作为模型；将模型导出为确定性C推理模块并编译进xApp二进制文件，消除外部机器学习运行时依赖；进行噪声消融、混淆矩阵分析和基于CDF的延迟特性表征。

Result: 逻辑回归推理延迟为1-5微秒，MLP为10-25微秒；端到端服务延迟保持在4ms以下；六模型对比显示监督模型准确率在0.88-0.90之间；两种嵌入模型在超过95%的循环执行中满足10ms Near-RT预算；验证了嵌入执行的可行性而非生产级泛化能力。

Conclusion: 轻量级AI模型可以在Near-RT RIC时序约束内运行，同时保持确定性执行；逻辑回归与MLP的相似性反映代理问题结构而非模型探索有限；研究证明了AI嵌入Near-RT RIC软件循环的技术可行性，为O-RAN智能控制提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) architectures introduce programmable Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controllers (Near-RT RICs) that support closed-loop control through xApps at timescales from 10 ms to 1 s. Although AI has been widely studied for RAN optimization, fewer works demonstrate measured AI inference embedded directly within the Near-RT RIC software loop on a live testbed. This paper presents an AI-enabled network-state classification xApp implemented on an OpenAirInterface (OAI) and FlexRIC testbed. The xApp is trained and evaluated on a structured synthetic dataset that emulates cross-layer RAN states using MAC, RLC, PDCP, GTP, and UE-count features. The results validate embedding and execution feasibility rather than production-level generalization. Logistic regression and a shallow multilayer perceptron (MLP) are exported as deterministic C inference modules and compiled into the xApp binary, eliminating external machine-learning runtime dependencies. Measured inference latency is 1 to 5 microseconds for logistic regression and 10 to 25 microseconds for the MLP, while end-to-end service latency remains below 4 ms. A six-model comparison shows that supervised models achieve similar accuracy, ranging from 0.88 to 0.90, indicating that LR and MLP similarity reflects the proxy problem structure rather than limited model exploration. Noise ablation, confusion-matrix analysis, and CDF-based latency characterization show that both embedded models satisfy the 10 ms Near-RT budget for more than 95% of projected loop executions. These results demonstrate that lightweight AI can operate within Near-RT RIC timing constraints while preserving deterministic execution. We also release RIC Workbench, a lightweight orchestration dashboard for reproducing the testbed on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [3] [CSI Simulation: Why Additive Noise Fails and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01882)
*Aymen Bouferroum,Ildi Alla,Vincent Lenders,Valeria Loscri*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了现有CSI数据仿真方法（AWGN）的假设缺陷，提出M_QTC模型显著提升仿真数据保真度，使下游任务性能接近真实数据水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSI数据仿真方法通常假设接收链路线性且增益不变，通过添加AWGN生成训练数据。然而在实际应用中，收集所有目标条件下的标注数据不可行，因此亟需验证该假设的有效性并改进仿真方法。

Method: 通过RF干扰作为可控扰动，在6种商用接收器和2个室内环境中实证测试接收链路假设。提出M_QTC测量校准模型，通过分位数映射、时域滤波和基于copula的跨子载波重排序学习每子载波分布变换。

Result: M_QTC将幅度误差降低8倍，在四个互补维度上弥合了89%的保真度差距。在下游任务中，5种不同分类器在M_QTC仿真数据上训练可恢复93%的真实数据干扰检测性能，而AWGN训练的分类器性能接近随机决策。

Conclusion: 接收链路线性增益不变的假设不成立，自动增益控制会压缩信道估计。M_QTC模型能有效弥合仿真与真实数据间的保真度差距，显著提升下游任务性能，为CSI数据仿真提供了更可靠的方法。

Abstract: Channel State Information (CSI) has become a widely used wireless channel sensing modality for applications such as indoor localization, activity recognition, and respiration monitoring. Because collecting labeled data under every target condition is impractical, training CSI-based models often relies on simulated data produced by adding noise or perturbations to recorded channel estimates, most commonly additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN). This practice assumes that the receiver chain between the antenna and the channel estimator is linear and gain-invariant. We test this assumption empirically using RF jamming as a controlled perturbation on 6 commodity receivers across 2 indoor environments. The assumption does not hold. Automatic gain control compresses the channel estimate multiplicatively before digitization, producing amplitude distributions that no additive noise variance can reproduce. To close the resulting fidelity gap, we propose M_QTC, a measurement-calibrated model that learns the per-subcarrier distribution transformation through quantile mapping, temporal filtering, and copula-based cross-subcarrier reordering. M_QTC reduces amplitude error 8-fold and closes 89% of the aggregate fidelity gap across four complementary dimensions. The improvement transfers directly to downstream tasks, where 5 classifiers from different families trained on M_QTC-simulated data recover 93% of real-data jamming detection performance, while AWGN-trained classifiers remain near random decision.

</details>

### [4] [HYPIC: Accelerating Hybrid-Attention LLM Serving with Position-Independent Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01299)
*Yifei Liu,Juntong Wu,Yang Liu,Junhao Hu,Minghao Li,Xiaoxu Chen,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Hypic，首个支持混合注意力LLM的位置无关缓存服务系统，通过引入segment-cumulative转换算子和边界接缝窗口重计算，实现了TTFT降低2.45倍、峰值吞吐量提升至多2.0倍的性能优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG和智能体LLM服务中，提示词由独立片段组成长上下文，导致预填充阶段主导计算成本。现有两种优化方向——位置无关缓存(PIC)和混合注意力模型——无法共存：将PIC应用于混合注意力模型会失败，因为逐token的KV缓存复用原语无法传递到逐请求的循环状态。

Method: 针对线性注意力层，识别出segment-cumulative转换算子作为缺失的代数原语，将其与每个片段的零起始终止状态一起缓存，实现独立缓存片段的近精确常量时间状态组合；针对剩余的全注意力层，发现最显著的注意力偏差集中在片段边界，因此只需在每个边界重计算小接缝窗口即可恢复跨片段回溯；利用片段级自包含性并行化缓存未命中的预填充。

Result: 在4个混合注意力模型和5个工作负载上评估，Hypic平均减少TTFT 2.45倍，峰值吞吐量提升至多2.0倍，同时保持与完全重计算相比3.3个精度点以内的准确性。

Conclusion: Hypic成功解决了混合注意力模型与位置无关缓存不兼容的问题，为RAG和智能体LLM服务提供了高效的长上下文处理方案，将原本导致尾部延迟的主要因素——长冷请求——转化为可加速的工作负载。

Abstract: In retrieval augmented generation (RAG) and agentic LLM serving, prompts are assembled from independent segments into long contexts, making the prefill stage dominate the per-request computation cost. To this cost, two directions have emerged in parallel: position-independent caching (PIC) admits KV reuse for non-contiguous segments shared across different requests, while hybrid-attention models reduce computation complexity by replacing most full-attention layers with linear attention. However, they cannot coexist: applying PIC to hybrid-attention models breaks down because per-token KV-cache reuse primitives do not transfer to the per-request recurrent state.
  In this work, we present Hypic, the first serving system for hybrid-attention LLMs with position-independent caching. For linear-attention layers, we identify the segment-cumulative transition operator as the missing algebraic primitive, and cache it alongside each segment's zero-start end-state, enabling near-exact and constant-time state composition of independently cached segments. For the remaining full-attention layers, existing PIC methods also fail as linear layers do not expose the per-token hidden states for selective recomputation. We show that the most significant attention deviation concentrates at segment boundaries, so recomputing only a small seam window at each boundary suffices to restore cross-segment lookback. Finally, Hypic exploits segment-level self-containment to parallelize cache-miss prefill across instances, turning long cold requests -- a major tail-latency contributor under both prefix caching and prior PIC -- into an accelerable workload. Evaluated across four hybrid-attention models and five workloads, Hypic reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) by 2.45x on average and improves peak throughput by up to 2.0x over existing systems, while staying within 3.3 points of full-recompute accuracy.

</details>


### [5] [SLFS: a Flexible, Low-Cost Distributed File System Using Serverless Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01486)
*Cheng Hao,Yang,Paola Alsharabaty,Soufiane Jounaid,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Ji-Yong Shin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SLFS是首个基于无服务器函数构建的分布式文件系统，通过创新的短生命周期多线程服务器设计克服冷启动问题，相比现有系统降低高达68%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式文件系统需要为峰值需求配置资源，但文件访问模式波动显著，导致非高峰期大量容量闲置。现有扩展机制以整个服务器为粒度，需要数分钟到数小时，无法跟踪文件系统常见的快速、细粒度负载变化。无服务器计算具有毫秒级弹性和按使用付费的特点，提供了有吸引力的替代方案。

Method: SLFS是首个将无服务器函数用于数据和元数据操作的分布式文件系统。主要技术包括：(1) 在键值存储之上实现文件服务，保持函数操作简单简短；(2) 引入新颖的多线程、短生命周期服务器设计，在维持低成本的同时克服冷启动问题；(3) 策略执行协调器高效地将文件映射到函数实例，弹性扩展系统，并控制函数生命周期以平衡性能和成本；(4) 可灵活运行在从S3等云原生服务到用户管理的键值存储等多样化存储后端上。

Result: SLFS相比基础无服务器设计将冷启动缓解580倍；相比λFS、EFS和Ceph，成本分别降低高达63%、68%和63%。

Conclusion: SLFS证明了无服务器架构可以有效构建分布式文件系统，在保持良好性能的同时显著降低成本，为云存储系统设计提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large-scale distributed file systems must provision resources for peak demand, yet file access patterns fluctuate significantly, leaving substantial capacity idle during off-peak periods. Existing scaling mechanisms operate at the granularity of entire servers and take minutes to hours, making them unable to track the rapid, fine-grained load variations that file systems commonly experience. Serverless computing, with its millisecond-granularity elasticity and pay-per-use pricing, offers a compelling alternative. We present SLFS, the first distributed file system built with serverless functions for both data and metadata operations. SLFS implements file services on top of key-value stores, keeping function operations simple and short, and introduces a novel multi-threaded, short-lived server design that overcomes the cold-start problem while maintaining low cost. A policy-enforcing coordinator efficiently maps files to function instances, scales the system elastically, and controls function lifetimes to balance performance and cost. SLFS can flexibly run on diverse storage backends -- from cloud-native services like S3 to user-managed key-value stores -- enabling configurable cost-performance trade-offs. Our evaluation shows that SLFS mitigates cold starts by 580$\times$ compared to the base serverless design and outperforms $λ$FS, EFS, and Ceph at up to 63%, 68%, and 63% lower cost, respectively.

</details>


### [6] [OmniPilot: An Uncertainty-Aware LLM Inference Advisor for Heterogeneous GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01579)
*D. Balamurugan,Thomas W. Bush*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了OmniPilot，一个用于LLM服务配置的启动顾问系统，通过保形校准的分位数成本模型和OOD弃权层，在异构GPU集群上实现了95%的top-1配置选择准确率和6.2%的吞吐量预测MAPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享异构GPU集群上服务大语言模型时，用户和运营商需要在投入宝贵节点时间之前选择GPU类型、张量并行度和精度。这些选择具有挑战性，因为有效吞吐量、启动成功率和集群需求利用率持续波动。静态配置方案错过了关键交互：量化效果严重依赖模型系列、键值缓存压力产生尺寸与精度权衡、不同张量并行度的故障率差异超过两倍。此外，集群资源经常受到不可预测硬件故障的限制。

Method: OmniPilot将保形校准的分位数成本模型（涵盖八个服务目标）与分布外(OOD)弃权层配对。该模型使用校准到运营商显式偏好的经济效用指标对配置进行排序，并在请求超出其测量支持范围时选择弃权。

Result: 在A100、H100和H200硬件上跨越四种精度的460次基准测试中，OmniPilot以6.2%的平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)和log空间R²=0.92预测聚合吞吐量。该顾问实现了95%的top-1准确率，平均效用遗憾仅为0.003。在不受支持单元的OOD保留集测试中，预测误差上升至24-46%，保形区间覆盖率为0/5，但弃权层成功将所有五个样本标记为低置信度。

Conclusion: OmniPilot成功解决了LLM服务配置选择的挑战，通过OOD弃权机制有效识别低置信度预测，避免了在不支持场景下的错误决策。随着时间推移，这些OOD场景将被整合到训练数据集中，以持续扩展顾问的支持范围，实现系统的自我改进。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) on a shared, heterogeneous GPU cluster requires users and operators to select the GPU type, tensor-parallel degree, and precision before committing valuable node-hours. Making these choices is challenging because effective throughput, launch-success rates, and cluster demand and utilization continuously fluctuate. Furthermore, static configuration recipes miss critical interactions: quantization effects depend heavily on the model family, key-value cache pressure creates size-by-precision trade-offs, and failure rates vary by more than twofold across different tensor-parallel degrees. Additionally, cluster resources are frequently constrained by unpredictable hardware failures. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{OmniPilot}, a launch advisor that predicts serving costs for feasible configurations and abstains when requests fall outside its measured support envelope. OmniPilot pairs a conformally calibrated quantile cost model (spanning eight serving targets) with an out-of-distribution (OOD) abstention layer. It ranks configurations using an economic utility metric calibrated to an operator's revealed preferences. In evaluations across 460 benchmark runs on A100, H100, and H200 hardware across four precisions, OmniPilot predicts aggregate throughput with a 6.2\% mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) and a log-space $R^2=0.92$. The advisor achieves 95\% top-1 accuracy with a mean utility regret of just 0.003. When tested on an OOD holdout of unsupported cells, prediction error climbs to 24-46\% and conformal intervals cover 0 of 5 points; however, the abstention layer successfully flags all five as low-confidence. Over time, these OOD scenarios will be integrated into the training dataset to continuously expand the advisor's support envelope.

</details>


### [7] [Arachne: Orchestrating Cascades for Efficient Text-to-Video Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01701)
*Peng Yu,Yuankai Fan,Yang Qiu,Tian Li,Bihuan Chen,Yin Chen,Qizhen Weng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Arachne，一种用于大规模文本到视频（T2V）模型训练的新型框架。它通过细粒度的“级联”分解和时空协同优化，解决了传统分桶策略在静态并行方案下的负载不平衡问题，将迭代时间减少了高达65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模文本到视频（T2V）模型的训练数据在分辨率和时长上具有高度异构性。现有的分桶（bucketing）训练策略结合静态并行方案（如数据和序列并行），在面对日益增长的计算和数据规模时，会导致严重的负载不平衡和硬件利用率低下。

Method: 提出Arachne框架，将训练过程分解为细粒度的计算单元，称为“级联”（cascades）。该框架通过协同的空间和时间优化，在集群中编排这些级联单元的分布式执行与同步。

Result: 全面评估表明，与领先框架相比，Arachne将迭代时间减少了高达65%，并展现出正向的扩展趋势，即随着训练规模的扩大，其性能优势进一步放大。

Conclusion: Arachne有效地解决了大规模异构视频数据训练中的负载不平衡问题，显著提升了硬件利用率和训练效率，证明了其在大型T2V模型训练扩展方面的优越性。

Abstract: The rising demand for AI-generated videos is fueled by advances in large-scale Text-to-Video (T2V) models, trained on extensive datasets of video clips spanning diverse resolutions and durations. To address this data heterogeneity, current training methods often use a bucketing strategy that groups samples into discrete buckets for efficiency. However, this approach struggles to scale with compute and data volumes under static parallelism schemes, such as data and sequence parallelism, leading to significant workload imbalances and hardware under-utilization.
  In this paper, we present Arachne, a novel training framework for efficient T2V model training at scale. Arachne decomposes the training process into fine-grained computational units, called \textit{cascades}, orchestrating their distributed execution and synchronization across the cluster through coordinated spatial and temporal optimization. Our comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that Arachne reduces iteration time by up to 65\% over leading frameworks, exhibiting a positive scaling trend where its performance advantages amplify as training scale grows.

</details>


### [8] [Exploiting Task-Based Parallelism for the Red-Black Gauss-Seidel Method on 2D Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01735)
*Shiting Long,Gustavo Ramirez-Hidalgo,Andreas Frommer,Dirk Pleiter*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对多色Gauss-Seidel迭代法，提出使用任务并行编程模型替代传统分治策略，在保持相当性能的同时提升对硬件异步性的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分治并行策略在实现多色Gauss-Seidel方法时面临全局同步开销和负载不均衡问题，限制了并行效率。

Method: 使用两种任务并行编程模型实现红黑Gauss-Seidel方法，并与传统分治并行实现进行对比评估；以2D泊松方程求解为基准测试。

Result: 任务并行实现能够达到与传统分治并行化相当的性能，同时对硬件级异步性具有更好的适应能力。

Conclusion: 任务并行方法为多色Gauss-Seidel提供了高效的并行化途径，可自然推广至非结构网格和宽模板产生的更一般多色方案。

Abstract: Gauss-Seidel is a well-established iterative method for the solution of linear systems, and multicoloring has been widely used to increase parallelism in iterative solution techniques. Implementing multi-color Gauss-Seidel with conventional divide-and-conquer parallelization strategies, however, may be inefficient due to global synchronization requirements and load imbalances. Task-based programming models can mitigate these issues by enabling fine-grained parallelism, removing global barriers and allowing updates of different colors to partially overlap in time. In this work, we implement the red-black Gauss-Seidel method using two task-based programming models and compare them with a classical divide-and-conquer parallel implementation to evaluate the impact of fine-grained parallelism on execution efficiency. The red-black scheme serves as a representative example, as task-based approaches naturally extend to more general multi-color schemes arising from unstructured grids and wider stencils. Using the solve of the 2D Poisson equation as benchmark, our results show that task-based implementations can achieve performance comparable to conventional divide-and-conquer parallelization while providing greater resilience to hardware-level asynchronicity.

</details>


### [9] [HCMS: Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline for Communication-Computation Overlap in Long-Sequence Parallel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01817)
*Chao Yuan,Pan Li,Yingnan Sun,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出HCMS方法，通过对注意力头进行分块和双CUDA流实现细粒度通信-计算重叠，在视频生成等长序列场景下相比Ulysses和Ring Attention分别获得10%-17.5%和5%-14.5%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全对全序列并行方法在处理中等长度序列时，通信与计算严格串行执行，导致硬件资源利用率不足。

Method: 利用多头注意力的计算独立性，将注意力头划分为多个块，通过双CUDA流实现细粒度的通信-计算重叠；该方法与FlashAttention和SDPA等现有优化正交兼容，无需修改底层内核，支持非均匀划分且保持数值等价。

Conclusion: HCMS有效解决了序列并行中通信-计算串行导致的资源利用率问题，为视频生成等长序列训练提供了高效的优化方案。

Abstract: All-to-all based sequence parallelism methods execute communication and computation strictly in serial when processing medium-long sequences, resulting in hardware resource underutilization. This paper proposes Head-Chunked Multi-Stream Pipeline (HCMS), which exploits the computational independence of multi-head attention by partitioning attention heads into multiple chunks and achieving fine-grained communication-computation overlap through dual CUDA streams. HCMS is orthogonally compatible with existing optimizations such as FlashAttention and SDPA, requires no modification to underlying kernels, supports uneven partitioning while maintaining numerical equivalence. Experiments validate the effectiveness across four GPU platforms at 2-8 GPU scales: for typical video generation sequence lengths of 31K-56K tokens, HCMS achieves 10\%-17.5\% speedup over the Ulysses baseline and 5\%-14.5\% speedup over Ring Attention; end-to-end acceleration of 6.8\% is achieved on the Wan2.2 model. Theoretical analysis shows that HCMS benefits are positively correlated with communication ratio $ρ$, and its use is recommended when $ρ>20\%$.

</details>

*Ohad Eitan,Idit Keidar,Ehud Shapiro*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对草根平台（基于自选公钥和智能手机的分布式代理系统）的容错安全机制，使其能够从私钥丢失和设备故障等重大故障中恢复。通过社交图谱、身份托管者和状态托管者的组合，实现了无全局资源依赖的去中心化身份恢复和状态恢复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 草根平台作为分布式系统缺乏全局资源支持，当用户遭遇私钥丢失或智能手机丢失/损坏等重大故障时，传统依赖中心化机构的恢复机制无法适用，因此需要设计一种基于对等网络的、无需全局资源的容错恢复方案。

Method: 提出三层机制：（1）草根社交图谱，代理在其中建立和维护友谊关系；（2）身份托管者，由每个人指定；（3）状态托管者，针对特定平台。当用户遭遇身份丢失时，在其身份托管者的绝对多数同意下，朋友节点负责在图谱中替换旧公钥并恢复友谊关系。技术实现上，将社交图谱及其安全版本形式化为守护式多代理原子事务，通过通信意愿代理（一种更接近实现的最终同步消息传递模型）实现，并证明实现能将可恢复故障的运行映射到规范的正确运行。

Result: 成功设计并形式化验证了一个完整的容错恢复框架：能够从身份丢失（私钥丢失）和设备丢失（私钥保留）两种故障中恢复；对于草根币和债券应用，展示了状态恢复的平台特定方面——货币的单写入者日志可以精确恢复，恢复后的主权实体能够继续运行而不发生双重支付问题。

Conclusion: 证明了在无全局资源的草根平台中，通过结合社交信任关系（朋友节点）和托管机制（身份/状态托管者），可以构建安全可靠的容错恢复系统，为去中心化平台的身份管理和状态恢复提供了可验证的理论基础和实现方案。

Abstract: We consider grassroots platforms -- distributed systems of agents consisting of people identified by self-chosen public keys and their machines (smartphones) -- and wish to make them secure against \emph{major faults}: the loss of their private keys and/or their smartphones. As grassroots platforms have no global resource to rely on for recovery, our peer-based solution is based on: (\ia) \emph{a grassroots social graph} in which agents establish and maintain friendships; (\ib) \emph{identity custodians}, designated by each person, and (\ic) \emph{state custodians}, which are grassroots platform-specific. Upon a person experiencing identity loss, and given a willing supermajority of the identity custodians of the person, the friends of the person replace the old public key with the new one across the graph and restore friendships, where all friends serve as state custodians for the social graph. Choosing a new keypair, obtaining a new smartphone, and convincing identity custodians to will a change of key all happen ``off-chain''. Recovery from machine loss without loss of key (e.g. smartphone run over by truck, or its memory wiped) is simpler, requiring only the help of state custodians.
  We specify the social graph and its secure version as guarded multiagent atomic transactions, and implement the secure social graph via communicating volitional agents, an eventually synchronous message-passing model one step closer to implementation. We prove the implementation maps runs with recoverable faults to correct runs of the specification.
  We follow a similar path for grassroots coins and bonds, showing a common core as well as the platform-specific aspects of state recovery: a currency's single-writer log is recovered exactly, the recovered sovereign resuming without double-spending.

</details>


### [11] [Lynx: Progressive Speculative Quantization for accelerating KV Transfer in Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01831)
*Wenchen Han,Gingfung Matthew Yeung,Marco Barletta,William Toner,Amory Hoste,Adam Barker*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Lynx提出了一种渐进式KV缓存传输系统，通过将缓存分为高优先级Anchor流和低优先级Residual流，实现了与激进量化相当的TTFT，同时保持了高精度推理的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理在LLM服务中日益普遍，需要在分离式推理架构中传输大量KV缓存。现有KV量化技术无法同时实现低网络延迟和高推理精度。传统方法假设KV缓存是不可分割的单位，必须完全接收后才能使用，这成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 利用KV缓存中不同位对注意力计算贡献不均等的特性（高有效位捕获注意力粗粒度结构，低有效位精化精度），将KV缓存分区为高优先级Anchor流（携带最高有效位）和低优先级Residual流（携带剩余精度）。解码在收到Anchor流后立即开始推测执行，同时并发传输Residual流，最后通过验证确保与高精度解码等价。

Result: 在多个模型和服务工作负载上，Lynx实现了与激进4-bit KV量化相当的TTFT，同时匹配BF16高精度推理的准确性；相比标准8-bit KV量化，TTFT提升最高1.43倍；相比最先进方法，准确性提升最高5.1%。

Conclusion: 通过打破KV缓存必须完整接收的传统假设，利用渐进式分流传输策略，Lynx成功在长上下文推理场景中同时实现了低延迟和高精度的目标，为分离式LLM服务提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly common in large language model (LLM) serving, driven by retrieval-augmented generation and agentic systems. In disaggregated inference, these workloads require transferring large Key-Value (KV) caches across the network, where decoding cannot begin until the transfer completes. Recent KV quantization techniques reduce data volume and alleviate this bottleneck, but existing schemes fail to achieve both low network-exposed latency and high inference accuracy.
  We challenge the assumption that the KV cache is an indivisible unit that must be fully received before use. We leverage the observation that different bits in the KV cache contribute unequally to attention computation and inference precision: the most significant bits capture the coarse structure of attention and the least significant bits refine precision. This property enables partial use of the KV cache during decoding. We present Lynx, a system that enables progressive, split-stream KV transfer by partitioning the KV cache into a high-priority Anchor stream carrying the most significant bits and a low-priority Residual stream carrying remaining precision. Decoding begins upon receipt of the Anchor stream and proceeds speculatively while the Residual stream is transferred concurrently, followed by verification that ensures equivalence to higher-precision decoding.
  Across multiple models and serving workloads, Lynx achieves Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) comparable to aggressive 4-bit KV quantization, while matching the accuracy of high-precision (BF16) inference, improving TTFT over standard 8-bit KV quantization by up to $1.43\times$ and improving accuracy over state-of-the-art by up to $5.1\%$.

</details>

*Xuan-Phi Nguyen,Shrey Pandit,Yiran Zhao,Semih Yavuz,Silvio Savarese,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MoP的内存高效训练框架，通过组合和特化多种并行技术，实现了在不到12个8×H200 GPU节点上进行万亿参数、百万token上下文长度的无损训练，相比FSDP2基线获得4.7-8.2倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练框架在处理大规模MoE模型时面临严峻的内存和通信瓶颈，特别是当模型规模达到万亿参数、上下文长度扩展到百万token时，传统方法无法满足内存需求，亟需一种能够在有限硬件资源下高效训练超大规模MoE模型的解决方案。

Method: 设计了一套内存高效的MoE训练技术栈，核心策略包括：(1) 根据MoE模型不同层和训练阶段的特点，组合并特化多种现有及新型并行技术；(2) 针对CPU、CPU内存、GPU HBM内存以及CPU-GPU、GPU-GPU、节点间通信带宽等物理约束进行优化；(3) 提出新颖的优化器步骤策略，实现高吞吐量与内存效率的平衡。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 在per-GPU吞吐量方面，相比经过强优化的FSDP2基线提升4.7x-8.2x，且规模越大优势越明显；(2) 成功支持高达1M token的上下文长度训练，而基线方法在64-128K长度时即耗尽内存；(3) 在不到12个8×H200 GPU节点上实现了万亿参数规模模型的无损预训练/微调，达到SOTA水平的吞吐量和内存效率。

Conclusion: MoP框架成功突破了大规模MoE模型训练的内存瓶颈，证明了通过精细化的并行策略组合和优化器设计，可以在有限的硬件资源上高效训练超大规模模型，为大语言模型的规模化训练提供了切实可行的技术方案。

Abstract: This paper showcases a memory-efficient training stack for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. It is a training paradigm that combines and specializes various existing and novel parallelism techniques at different layers and stages of the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model training pipeline. It leverages these techniques to achieve maximal efficiency given the physical constraints of CPU, CPU memory, GPU HBM memory, and the CPU-GPU, GPU-GPU, and node-node communication bandwidth of the GPU cluster. It also contains a novel strategy for the optimizer step to achieve high throughput and memory efficiency, enabling practitioners to conduct lossless pre-training/fine-tuning of trillion-parameter scale models, at a million context length, with just under 12 8x H200 GPU nodes, with state-of-the-art throughput and memory efficiency. In our experiments, MoP delivers 4.7x--8.2x higher per-GPU throughput than a strongly-tuned FSDP2 baseline (with the gap widening at larger scale) and sustains training at context lengths up to 1M tokens, where the baseline runs out of memory beyond 64--128K.

</details>


### [13] [Towards Load-Aware Prefill Deflection for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02043)
*Shrikara Arun,Anjaly Parayil,Srikant Bharadwaj,Renee St. Amant,Victor Rühle*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种主动的前缀偏转调度器，通过让解码节点以分块方式交错执行前缀阶段，解决了分离式LLM服务中前缀节点饱和而解码节点计算资源利用率低的问题，显著降低了P95 TTFT并提高了SLO达成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分离式LLM服务中，前缀和解码阶段运行在不同的GPU池上。然而在实际的突发、重尾工作负载下，前缀节点会饱和，而解码节点的计算资源却利用率不足。在生产环境的A100集群（2个前缀节点和2个解码节点）中，前缀执行仅占P95 TTFT的2-23%，排队和节点间GPU-GPU KV缓存传输占据了其余时间。这种不对称性导致了系统瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种主动的前缀偏转调度器。该调度器允许解码节点以分块前缀步骤的形式交错执行请求的前缀阶段，与正在进行的解码批次交替进行。对于每个排队的请求，估计其在前缀节点上的TTFT，并在每个解码节点上搜索最大的分块调度方案，确保正在进行的解码操作保持在TBT SLO范围内。当解码路径有助于降低尾部延迟时，将请求偏转到解码节点。由于偏转请求的前缀阶段在解码节点上就地运行，因此消除了节点间的KV传输。该方法基于vLLM实现，并使用DeepSeek-V2-Lite进行评估。

Result: 在生产式工作负载轨迹上进行评估，与最先进的分离式调度器相比，该方法将P95 TTFT降低了高达81%，SLO达成率提高了高达79%，且每个请求的路由开销低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: 本文提出的主动前缀偏转调度器有效地解决了分离式LLM服务中的资源利用不对称问题，通过智能地将前缀任务偏转到具有可用计算资源的解码节点，消除了昂贵的节点间KV传输，显著改善了系统的尾部延迟和服务质量。该方法在保持低路由开销的同时，大幅提升了性能指标，为生产环境中的LLM服务优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM serving runs prefill and decode on separate GPU pools to keep the two phases from interfering. In practice, this creates a new asymmetry: under bursty, heavy-tailed workloads prefill nodes saturate while decode nodes have compute underutilized, and on a production-style A100 cluster with 2 prefill and 2 decode nodes (2P2D), we find that prefill execution accounts for only 2-23% of P95 Time-to-First-Token (TTFT). Queuing and inter-node GPU-GPU KV-cache transfer account for the rest. We present a proactive prefill-deflecting scheduler that lets decode nodes serve prefill phase of requests as chunked-prefill steps interleaved with their in-flight decode batches. For each queued request, we estimate the TTFT it would see on the prefill node, and on every decode node, search for the largest chunk schedule that keeps in-flight decodes within their Time-Between-Tokens (TBT) SLO and deflect when the decode path helps tail latency. Because the prefill phase of deflected requests runs in place on the decode node, the inter-node KV transfer is eliminated. Implemented on vLLM and evaluated on production-style traces with DeepSeek-V2-Lite, our approach reduces P95 TTFT by upto 81% and raises SLO attainment by upto 79% over state-of-the-art disaggregated schedulers, at sub-millisecond per-request routing cost.

</details>


### [14] [Cadence: Extreme Pipelining with Multiple Concurrent Proposers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02275)
*Fatima Elsheimy,Mohammad Mussadiq Jalalzai,Tobias Klenze,Jovan Komatovic,Mike Setrin,Victor Shoup,Kushal Babel,Lioba Heimbach,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Cadence是首个实现任意低区块间隔、最优容错和最优快路径延迟的多提案者拜占庭容错共识协议，通过“极端流水线”解耦区块间隔与网络延迟，并在多提案者场景下提供短期抗审查和隐藏性，200个验证者环境下最终确认平均仅需219ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识协议存在两大局限：1）区块间隔受限于网络延迟，无法实现任意低延迟；2）单领导者对交易包含和排序形成垄断，可能导致审查或交易延迟。本文旨在解决这两个问题，实现多提案者场景下的低延迟、抗审查共识。

Method: 提出Cadence协议，采用三大核心技术：1）极端流水线（extreme pipelining）：时间划分为等间隔slot，每个slot独立运行共识实例，区块不直接依赖前置区块；2）多并发提案者（MCP）：每个slot由多个验证者提案，保证正确提案者的交易不会被审查或延迟；3）通用框架：将满足slot-consensus规范的一次性共识转换为多轮协议。实现包括Chorus（三轮快路径最终确认，两轮投机确认）和Conductor（slot编排器，异步时减速以限制开放slot数量）。

Result: 1）理论上证明在部分同步模型下满足安全性、活性、抗审查性和隐藏性，容错能力达到最优（n=3f+1）；2）实验验证：在Monad的200个验证者、每slot 5个提案者环境下，最终确认平均219ms（投机确认167ms），100ms区块间隔下交易平均等待50ms进入提案；3）成为首个在快路径延迟上达到单领导者共识性能的多提案者协议。

Conclusion: Cadence成功实现了多提案者共识的突破性性能：任意低区块间隔、最优容错和最优快路径延迟，同时保证了短期抗审查和隐藏性，为区块链系统提供了更公平、高效的共识解决方案。

Abstract: We present Cadence, a Byzantine fault-tolerant multi-proposer consensus protocol with arbitrarily low block intervals, optimal resilience, and optimal fast-path latency. Cadence divides time into equally spaced slots, one block per slot, each finalized in its own consensus instance. Blocks do not build directly on their predecessor, so instances run independently and none waits for an earlier block to finish or propagate; we call this extreme pipelining, decoupling the block interval from network latency. Cadence also removes the single-leader monopoly over transaction inclusion and ordering: under multiple concurrent proposers (MCP), several validators propose for each block, and it guarantees that, under synchrony, a transaction a correct proposer includes cannot be censored or deferred (short-term censorship resistance), and that no proposer can craft its proposal in reaction to the others' (hiding). To realize extreme pipelining, we introduce a general framework that turns any one-shot consensus meeting our slot-consensus specification into a multi-shot protocol. We instantiate it for MCP with two protocols of our own: Chorus, a slot consensus whose fast path finalizes a block in an optimal three rounds, with speculative finality one round earlier, and Conductor, an orchestrator that opens slots at an even cadence, more slowly under asynchrony to keep open slots bounded. To our knowledge, Cadence is the first MCP protocol to provide short-term censorship resistance and hiding at the fast-path latency of single-leader consensus. We prove safety, liveness, censorship resistance, and hiding under partial synchrony with optimal resilience (n = 3f+1). In simulation over Monad's 200 validators with five proposers per slot, finalization averages 219 ms (167 ms to speculative finality); at a 100 ms block interval a transaction waits on average 50 ms to enter a proposal.

</details>


### [15] [Elasticity in Parallel Sparse Triangular Solve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02324)
*Raphael S. Steiner,Christos K. Matzoros,Pál András Papp,Toni Böhnlein,A. N. Yzelman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为stale synchronous parallel的并行执行模式，用于稀疏三角线性方程组求解，并开发了ElasticDivide调度器，在48核ARM和x86机器上分别实现了7-30%和19-60%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在并行稀疏三角线性系统求解中，同步开销是主要性能瓶颈。传统的同步并行模式需要等待所有依赖任务完成后才能继续执行，导致计算资源闲置。因此，本文旨在设计一种新的执行模式，允许同步与计算重叠，从而提高并行效率。

Method: 本文提出了stale synchronous parallel执行模式，允许部分计算使用稍旧的数据进行，从而实现同步与计算的重叠。同时设计了一个通用的有向无环图(DAG)调度器ElasticDivide，能够生成此类调度。该调度器可以动态平衡并行度与数据一致性之间的权衡。

Result: 在48核ARM机器上，ElasticDivide相比最先进的同步调度器GrowLocal获得7-30%的几何平均加速；在48核x86机器上，相比SpMP获得19-60%的几何平均加速。实验结果表明该方法在不同硬件平台上均能有效提升性能。

Conclusion: Stale synchronous parallel模式为并行稀疏三角求解提供了一种有效的性能优化方案。通过允许同步与计算重叠，该方法在不显著牺牲求解精度的情况下，实现了显著的性能提升，为稀疏线性代数计算中的同步优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce stale synchronous parallel as a mode of execution in parallel sparse triangular linear system solve and present a general directed-acyclic-graph scheduler capable of producing such schedules. Stale-synchronous-parallel schedules allow the overlap of synchronisation and compute which results in a geometric-mean speed-up of $7$-$30\%$ of our scheduler, ElasticDivide, over state-of-the-art synchronous scheduler GrowLocal on an ARM machine using 48 cores. On an x86 machine using 48 cores, we report geometric-mean speed-ups of $19$-$60\%$ over SpMP.

</details>


### [16] [WattGPU: Predicting Inference Power and Latency on Unseen GPUs and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02391)
*Mauricio Fadel Argerich,Jonathan Fürst,Marta Patiño-Martínez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出WattGPU框架，仅利用公开的LLM元数据和GPU规格，无需硬件访问即可预测GPU功耗和延迟，在未见过的GPU和LLM上分别实现≤13.5%和≤8.5%的误差，优于传统基准方法约2-4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理工作负载正快速增长，成为数据中心能耗的重要来源。优化部署需要将特定LLM匹配到最高效的GPU，但现有方法需要逐个组合进行详尽性能分析，且现有预测模型仍需要性能分析数据，难以泛化到训练时未见过的硬件。

Method: 提出WattGPU框架，包含两个预测模型：平均GPU功耗模型和Token间延迟(ITL)模型。该方法仅利用公开可用的LLM元数据和GPU规格，无需硬件访问或性能分析。使用留一GPU和留一LLM交叉验证方法，在42个开源LLM(0.1B-27B参数)和8个GPU数据集上进行评估，覆盖离线和服务器两种场景。

Result: 功耗模型在未见GPU上，离线场景中位数绝对百分比误差≤3.4%，服务器场景≤13.5%；延迟模型在服务器模式误差≤8.5%，服务器场景GPU排名相关性Kendall τ≥0.76。相比负载缩放TDP功耗基准和roofline延迟基准，在服务器场景未见LLM-GPU组合上误差降低约4倍，在完全未见GPU上降低约2倍。

Conclusion: WattGPU成功实现了无需硬件访问的LLM-GPU性能预测，具有良好的泛化能力，可为数据中心运营商提供有效的GPU选择工具，显著降低预测误差，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference workloads are a rapidly growing contributor to data center energy consumption. Optimizing these deployments requires matching specific LLMs to the most efficient GPUs, but operators currently lack the tools to do so without exhaustively profiling each combination. While some predictive models exist, they still require profiling data and struggle to generalize to hardware unseen during training. To address this, we introduce \textit{WattGPU}, featuring two predictive models for mean GPU power draw and Inter-Token Latency (ITL). Our approach leverages only publicly available LLM metadata and GPU specifications, eliminating the need for hardware access or profiling while enabling generalization to unseen NVIDIA server-grade GPUs and LLMs. We evaluate our models using rigorous leave-one-GPU-out and leave-one-LLM-out cross-validation on a dataset of 42 open-source LLMs (0.1B--27B parameters) and 8 GPUs under both offline and server scenarios. The mean power draw model achieves a median absolute percentage error of $\leq3.4\%$ for offline and $\leq13.5\%$ for server scenarios on unseen GPUs, while the latency model achieves $\leq8.5\%$ in server mode, both maintaining strong GPU ranking correlations for server scenarios (Kendall $τ\geq0.76$). Compared to standard physically grounded baselines -- Load-Scaled Thermal Design Power (TDP) for power draw and roofline for latency -- our models reduce median absolute percentage error by approximately 4$\times$ on unseen LLM-GPU combinations for server scenarios or approximately 2$\times$ for completely unseen GPUs. WattGPU's data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/maufadel/wattgpu.

</details>


### [17] [FlintKV: A Fast Durable Storage Engine for Modern Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02401)
*Sergey Egorov,Gregory Chockler,Brijesh Dongol,Dan O'Keeffe,Sadegh Keshavarzi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlintKV是一个针对非易失性内存(NVM)优化的跳表存储引擎，原生支持生产级键值存储的完整API（快照、一致性迭代器、原子批处理），通过新型扁平合并并发控制算法实现了高达75%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NVM键值存储虽然在摄入和点查询方面实现了高吞吐量，但缺少或未充分支持现代数据库所需的更丰富的接口保证。生产级键值引擎（如RocksDB）提供的时间点快照、一致性迭代器和原子批处理等功能对于实现事务和并发控制至关重要，而现有NVM存储对这些功能支持不足。

Method: 提出了FlintKV，一个基于跳表的NVM优化存储引擎。核心技术包括：(1) 原生支持生产级键值存储的完整API；(2) 支持原子批处理写入和快照一致性迭代；(3) 保证持久化线性化；(4) 提出了一种新颖的基于扁平合并(flat-combining)的并发控制算法，利用多版本控制和精心协同设计的持久化机制来确保高性能和可扩展性；(5) 可独立部署或将其持久化跳表集成到现有NVM存储中。

Result: 实验评估表明，FlintKV在端到端吞吐量方面相比先前工作实现了高达75%的性能提升，同时完整支持生产环境所需的关键特性。

Conclusion: FlintKV成功填补了现有NVM键值存储在生产级功能支持方面的空白，证明了可以在不牺牲性能的前提下实现完整的API支持，为NVM存储引擎的设计提供了新的思路和实践参考。

Abstract: Byte-addressable non-volatile memory (NVM) offers an opportunity to rethink storage engine architectures. While recent NVM key-value stores achieve high throughput for ingestion and point lookups, they omit or under-specify the support for the richer interface guarantees required by modern databases. Production key-value engines (e.g., RocksDB) provide point-in-time snapshots, consistent iterators, and atomic batches-features essential for implementing transactions and concurrency control. We present FlintKV, an NVM-optimized skiplist-based storage engine that natively supports the full API of production key-value stores. FlintKV supports both atomic batch writes and snapshot-consistent iteration efficiently while guaranteeing durable linearizability. FlintKV can be deployed standalone or its durable skiplist can be integrated into existing NVM stores to enhance their capabilities. Central to FlintKV is a novel flat-combining based concurrency control algorithm that leverages multi-versioning and carefully co-designed persistence mechanisms to ensure high performance and scalability. Our empirical evaluation shows that FlintKV can achieve up to a 75% improvement in end-to-end throughput over prior work.

</details>

### [18] [Cache Merging as a Convergent Replicated State for Multi-Agent Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01308)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出CanonicalMerge方法，通过基于内容的排序实现多智能体KV-cache的确定性合并，解决了现有BagMerge方法的输入顺序依赖性问题，在保持精度的同时提供了结构化的合并保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体潜在推理中的BagMerge方法（通过RoPE重编码沿序列轴连接缓存）具有非交换性，其最佳输入顺序不可预测且随机制、潜在步骤预算和模型规模变化，导致合并结果不稳定。

Method: 提出CanonicalMerge方法：首先通过中间层的平均K-norm对缓存进行内容排序，使合并后的缓存在任意输入排列下字节完全一致（已在Qwen3-1.7B和4B上验证）；其次将复制状态与解码时布局分离，构建基于内容寻址潜在片段的集合作为CvRDT状态（具有交换性、结合性、幂等性、吸收性），CanonicalMerge作为其确定性渲染。

Result: 在分区推理基准测试中，CanonicalMerge在每个机制-预算-排序单元中均匹配最佳BagMerge排序结果，无需预知最佳顺序，仅以统计上不显著的精度损失换取无条件结构保证；在HotpotQA多文档问答任务中表现良好，而最接近的无训练输出融合基线（PackLLM）在匹配预算下落后45分。

Conclusion: CanonicalMerge成功解决了多智能体KV-cache合并的顺序依赖性问题，在保持精度的同时实现了确定性的缓存级合并，这与输出级融合形成明显区分，为k>2情况下的潜在轨迹组合提供了研究基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent latent reasoning composes agents' KV-caches into one context for a final agent. Prior work (Agent Primitives) does this by concatenating caches along the sequence axis with RoPE re-encoding, which we call BagMerge. BagMerge is non-commutative, and the best input ordering is unpredictable, shifting with the regime, the latent-step budget, and the model scale. We make this exchange a convergent replicated state. First, CanonicalMerge fixes the layout by content: ordering caches by mean K-norm at a middle layer renders the merged cache byte-identical under any input permutation, verified algorithmically (arity N<=5) and bit-for-bit on real Qwen3-1.7B and 4B state. Second, we separate the replicated state from decode-time layout: the state is a set of content-addressed latent fragments whose merge is set union, a state-based CvRDT (commutative, associative, idempotent, absorbing), and CanonicalMerge is its deterministic render. Because the render is byte-equivalent, every N=2 accuracy number carries over unchanged and re-delivered duplicates are absorbed rather than re-concatenated. On a partitioned-reasoning benchmark, CanonicalMerge matches the best BagMerge ordering in every regime-by-budget-by-ordering cell without knowing which order is best, trading a small, statistically insignificant accuracy margin for an unconditional structural guarantee. The behaviour transfers to real multi-document QA (HotpotQA), while the closest training-free output-fusion baseline (PackLLM) loses by 45 points at matched budget, placing cache-level merging in a regime distinct from output-level fusion. Finally, at k>2 the approach transports and colocates latent traces but does not by itself compose them, which we characterize to motivate future work.

</details>


### [19] [Simulation Based Reward Function Validation for Multi-Agent On Orbit Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01367)
*Patrick Quinn,Bala Prenith Reddy Gopu,George M. Nehma,Madhur Tiwari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三维重建分析的广义奖励函数，用于多智能体强化学习（MARL）控制的航天器在轨检查任务，使智能体能够自主决定图像采集时机并评估任意位置的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MARL航天器检查任务的奖励函数主要关注于到达目标周围有限的预定检查点，缺乏灵活性。为了提高检查效率和适应性，需要开发一种更通用的奖励函数，能够评估任意位置和数量的图像采集。

Method: 通过对轨道中被检查物体的三维重建进行分析，设计了一种广义奖励函数。该奖励函数能够评估任意数量的图像在任意位置的采集质量，并赋予训练好的智能体完全控制图像采集时机的自主权。

Result: 开发出的广义奖励函数使MARL智能体能够自主决定何时采集图像，而不仅限于预定检查点。研究结果为MARL检查任务提供了最佳实践的见解，并为更广泛的检查任务提供了有价值的参考。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅改进了特定的MARL检查任务，还为非MARL背景下的检查任务提供了重要的指导原则和经验总结。

Abstract: A proposed method for the control of groups of inspection spacecraft is Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL). While MARL has already been employed for this purpose in previous work, the reward functions used focus on reaching a finite set of predetermined inspection points around the target. In this work, we study and develop a generalized reward function for the MARL inspection task informed by the analysis of 3D reconstructions of inspected objects in orbit. Because the reward function is generalized such that any number of images at arbitrary locations may evaluated, we also allow trained agents to have complete control over when images are collected. With this approach, we gather insights into best practices for not only the specific MARL inspection task, but also gain key takeaways informative to the broader inspection task outside of a MARL context.

</details>


### [20] [Congestion-Based Slot Pricing in a Railway Auction Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01822)
*Bill Roungas,Sebastiaan Meijer*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文设计了一个用于研究稀缺资源分配的多智能体系统，结合拥堵定价和不对称调整机制，试图缓解大型运营商的策略性优势，但实验表明仅靠纠正性定价不足以完全中和这种优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于铁路放松管制下的时刻表分配问题，旨在解决异质策略性智能体之间离散、拥堵资源的分配难题，特别是大型运营商可能利用资源优势进行策略性主导的问题。

Method: 构建了一个基于Web的实时多智能体实验环境，人类参与者控制具有不同规模和容量的运营商智能体。系统采用重复博弈模型，设计了包含拥堵基础价格和不对称纠正调整的拍卖机制：对请求最多时段的智能体施加惩罚，对请求最少的给予奖励。

Result: 来自两场领域专家参与的结构化会议的探索性观察表明：拥堵机制按设计响应总需求，纠正激励被有效触发；但代表大型运营商的智能体尽管面临惩罚仍坚持高请求策略，说明纠正性定价虽必要但不足以中和策略性优势。事后复盘显示，参与者决策由所扮演的角色驱动，存在维持市场存在感和提高竞争对手成本等策略动机。

Conclusion: 纠正性定价机制对于缓解策略性优势是必要的但不够充分。研究为非对称预算下的多智能体机制设计提供了启示，指出了分析性验证和更大规模多智能体实验的未来研究方向。

Abstract: We present a multi-agent system for studying the allocation of discrete, congested resources among heterogeneous strategic agents, motivated by the problem of railway slot allocation under deregulation. Multiple operator-agents, differing in size and capacity, interact through a shared auction mechanism over repeated rounds under time-constrained decision-making. The mechanism combines a congestion-based base price that increases with aggregate demand with an asymmetric corrective adjustment that penalises the agent requesting the most slots and rewards the agent requesting the fewest, and is designed to mitigate strategic dominance by large agents while preserving transparency and congestion sensitivity. We formulate the interaction as a repeated game with incomplete information and implement the system as a real-time, web-based multi-agent environment in which human participants control individual agents and observe live marginal-cost and competitor feedback.
  We report exploratory observations from two structured sessions with domain experts acting as operator-agents. The congestion mechanism responds to aggregate demand as designed and the corrective incentives are actively triggered, but agents representing large operators persist with high-request strategies despite the penalty, suggesting that corrective pricing is necessary but not sufficient to neutralise strategic dominance in this multi-agent setting. A post-session debrief indicates that participants' decisions were driven by the assumed agent role rather than personal disposition, and provides qualitative support for strategic motives, such as preserving market presence and raising rivals' costs, operating alongside short-term profit maximisation. We discuss implications for multi-agent mechanism design under asymmetric budgets and outline directions for analytical validation and larger-scale multi-agent experiments.

</details>


### [21] [CausalSteward: An Agentic Divide-Conquer-Combine Copilot for Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01936)
*Nicholas Tagliapietra,Gian Lorenzo Marchioni,Moritz Willig,Juergen Luettin,Lavdim Halilaj,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了CAST框架，一种人机协作的多智能体系统，通过分治策略、融合先验知识与数据驱动方法来解决高维数据的因果发现问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从高维数据中学习因果模型面临巨大挑战，尤其在现实场景中核心假设常被违反导致因果可识别性问题。虽然存在大量包含有价值因果信息的先验知识，但如何将其有效整合到因果发现过程中仍是一个未解决的问题。

Method: 提出CausalSTeward (CAST)，一个新颖的人机协作框架。该系统是一个多智能体协作系统，采用分治策略，迭代地将大规模变量簇进行划分并分别分析。框架通过检索增强生成（RAG）和条件独立性测试等定制工具，将先验知识与数据驱动方法相融合。

Result: 该框架能够交互式地组装大规模因果模型，有效处理高维因果发现问题，并探讨了人机协作如何促进准确且可信的结果。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅提供了一个实用的高维因果发现解决方案，还深入分析了多智能体框架中因果推理的能力与局限性，以及人机协作在提升结果准确性与可信度方面的价值。

Abstract: Learning causal models from high-dimensional data is a significant challenge, particularly in real-world settings where violations of core assumptions lead to causal identifiability issues. Although massive amounts of prior knowledge are available, and contain valuable causal information, effectively integrating this knowledge into the causal discovery process remains an open problem. We introduce CausalSTeward (CAST), a novel human-in-the-loop framework for interactively assembling large causal models. CausalSteward is a multi-agent collaborative system that tackles high-dimensional causality through a divide-and-conquer approach where large clusters of variables are iteratively partitioned and then separately analyzed. Our framework fuses prior knowledge with a data-driven approach by using tailored tools such as retrieval augmented generation and conditional independence tests. Finally, we use this work to examine the capabilities and limitations of causal reasoning in multi-agent frameworks, and how the human-in-the-loop can contribute to accurate and trustworthy results.

</details>


### [22] [AgentsCAD: Automated Design for Manufacturing of FDM Parts via Multi-Agent LLM Reasoning and Geometric Feature Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02448)
*Emmanuel George,Christopher Keefe,Peter Pak,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgentsCAD系统，一个结合B-Rep几何与LLM推理的多智能体框架，用于自动化FDM零件的可制造性设计（DFM）修改，解决了切片软件无法修改几何的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDM制造的零件通常需要DFM修改以保证可打印性和结构完整性，但现有切片软件只能检测缺陷（如陡峭悬垂）而无法修改底层几何。

Method: 系统解析STEP文件后，检测超过45°阈值的悬垂，构建面邻接拓扑图，可选注入GraphSAGE模型（在MFCAD++数据集上训练）的语义特征标签，然后调度Claude Sonnet设计推理智能体推荐修改方案，最后由GPT-4o视觉语言验证器检查几何完整性。

Result: 在鸟屋模型的测试案例中，系统正确诊断了悬垂缺陷，选择了合适的缓解策略，并提出了物理有效的修正方案，输出了修改后的STEP文件和可读报告。

Conclusion: AgentsCAD部分解决了LLM驱动CAD修改中几何到语言转换的核心问题，为自动化DFM提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Parts manufactured with Fused Deposition Modeling (FDM) often require Design for Additive Manufacturing (DFAM) modifications to ensure printability, structural integrity, and reduced post-processing. Current slicers identify defects such as steep overhangs but are unable to modify the underlying geometry. This work presents AgentsCAD, a multi-agent system that bridges raw boundary-representation (B-Rep) geometry and Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning to automate targeted DFM. The workflow begins by parsing a STEP file. The agentic system detects overhangs above a 45°threshold, constructs a face-adjacency topology graph, and optionally injects semantic feature labels from a GraphSAGE model trained on MFCAD++ (59,665 parts), before dispatching a Claude Sonnet design-reasoning agent that recommends reorientations, fillets, chamfers, and similar modifications. A GPT-4o vision-language verifier inspects rendered views to confirm geometric integrity. Outputs include a modified STEP file and a human-readable report. A test case on a birdhouse model demonstrates that the system correctly diagnoses overhangs, selects appropriate defect mitigation strategies, and proposes physically valid corrections, partially solving the geometry-to-language translation problem central to LLM-driven CAD modification.

</details>


### [23] [Adoption and Ecosystem Health: A Longitudinal Analysis of Open-Source Multi-Agent Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02453)
*Xi Zhang,Papi Menon,Vivian Chu,Koray Cosguner*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究发现GitHub星标数作为AI智能体框架流行度指标不可靠，提出贡献者密度、跨生态参与度和留存率作为更稳健的评估指标。LangChain充当共享基础设施，吸引82.5%的跨生态贡献者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自ChatGPT发布以来，开源AI智能体框架激增，工程团队在框架选择时面临困难。现有选择标准依赖GitHub星标等流行度信号，但这些信号可能受炒作周期和非有机活动影响，无法真实反映生态系统健康状况。

Method: 分析2022年末至2026年初15个主要开源AI智能体框架仓库，使用808,042个星标、73,997个拉取请求、86,241次提交和987,330个用户画像数据，从认知度、采用率和留存率三个维度评估生态系统健康状况。引入贡献者密度（每千星标的贡献者数量）作为关键指标，并进行跨框架贡献分析。

Result: 发现一：头条流行度不可靠。星标数反映炒作周期和非有机活动。AutoGPT单月获得111,967星标但贡献者密度低于9，而LangChain为41。Pydantic-AI等低调框架贡献者密度更高。发现二：认知度与采用率分离。MetaGPT和LangFlow高可见度但贡献者密度比率低于5。Openai-agents-python的有限贡献者基础表明仅靠机构支持不足以确保社区深度。LangChain吸引82.5%的跨生态贡献者，充当共享基础设施。发现三：留存率在首次贡献后30天内下降最陡峭，90天后趋于稳定。

Conclusion: 生态系统健康应通过贡献者密度、跨生态参与度和留存率衡量，而非仅依赖星标数。这些指标为工程团队提供了更稳健的框架评估基础，有助于做出更明智的技术选择决策。

Abstract: Since ChatGPT's launch in November 2022, open-source agentic frameworks have proliferated, making framework selection important for engineering teams while obscured by popularity signals such as GitHub stars. This paper analyzes 15 major open-source AI agent framework repositories from late 2022 to early 2026, using 808,042 stars, 73,997 pull requests, 86,241 commits, and 987,330 user profiles to assess ecosystem health across awareness, adoption, and retention. Three findings emerge. First, headline popularity is unreliable. Star counts reflect hype cycles and inorganic activity. AutoGPT gained 111,967 stars in one month but converted fewer than 9 contributors per 1,000 stars, defined as contributor density in this research, compared with LangChain's 41. Lower-profile frameworks such as Pydantic-AI show higher contributor density, indicating deeper adoption. Second, mapping awareness against adoption shows that visibility and engagement diverge. MetaGPT and LangFlow have contributor density ratios below 5 even with their high visibility. Openai-agents-python's limited contributor base suggests institutional backing alone does not ensure community depth. By analyzing cross-framework contribution, we discover that LangChain functions as a shared infrastructure, attracting 82.5% of cross-ecosystem contributors. Third, retention drops most steeply in the first 30 days of initial contribution and stabilizes near 90 days. Overall, ecosystem health is better measured by contributor density, cross-ecosystem engagement, and retention than by stars alone. These metrics offer teams a more robust basis for framework evaluation.

</details>

### [24] [Criticality-Based Guard Rail Validation for AI Agent Decisions in Autonomous Telecom Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02210)
*Ravi Kant Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRV框架，一种可在运行时拦截并验证AI决策的可标准化架构，通过多维度评估决策关键性并分级验证，防止错误决策影响电信网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向全自主电信网络（自治网络4-5级）演进过程中，AI/ML智能体需要在无人工干预下实时决策，但缺乏标准化运行时机制来拦截和验证单个推理输出，存在自主决策错误的风险。

Method: 提出Guard Rail Validation (GRV)框架，在决策执行前对其进行拦截和验证。框架跨多个加权维度（包括动作范围、动作类型、服务关键性、智能体自治级别、可逆性和时间行为模式）评估决策以确定关键性等级。基于该等级，采用分级验证机制：带日志执行、边界检查、独立智能体验证或多智能体共识。框架还提供跨智能体冲突检测（带关键性加权优先级解决）以及面向监管合规（如欧盟AI法案第14条）的运行时合规日志记录。

Result: 提出了框架架构、算法流程和O-RAN部署模型，并针对电信领域已知AI/ML攻击评估了威胁覆盖范围。

Conclusion: GRV框架为自主电信网络中的AI决策提供了标准化、可部署的运行时安全验证机制，有效降低错误自主决策风险并支持监管合规。

Abstract: The evolution toward fully autonomous telecommunications networks (Autonomous Network Levels 4-5) requires AI/ML agents to make real-time network decisions without human intervention. However, no standardized runtime mechanism exists to intercept and validate individual inference outputs before they trigger live network state changes, creating risks of erroneous autonomous decisions. This paper proposes the Guard Rail Validation (GRV) framework, a standardizable runtime architecture for intercepting and validating AI-driven decisions before execution. The framework evaluates decisions across multiple weighted dimensions -- including action scope, action type, service criticality, agent autonomy level, reversibility, and temporal behavioural patterns -- to determine a criticality level. Based on this level, graduated validation mechanisms are applied: execute-with-logging, bounds checking, independent agent validation, or multi-agent consensus. The framework additionally provides cross-agent conflict detection with criticality-weighted priority resolution and runtime conformance logging for regulatory compliance (e.g., EU AI Act Article 14). We present the architecture, algorithmic procedures, O-RAN deployment model, and evaluate threat coverage against known AI/ML attacks in telecommunications.

</details>


### [25] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PACE框架，通过神经符号AI结合神经网络预测模型与符号推理层（如ASP），生成符合领域知识和可行性约束的反事实解释，解决了现有方法生成的建议不现实或不可行的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽然能生成改变预测结果的输入变化，但往往缺乏对领域知识和干预约束的显式建模机制，导致生成的建议不现实或不可行，无法提供可操作的决策支持。

Method: 提出PACE模块化神经符号框架，将预测与推理分离：使用神经网络分类器进行预测，利用符号推理层（Answer Set Programming）编码并强制执行领域特定约束，在生成反事实解释时明确建模可行的干预措施。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明，符号约束能够生成更好地满足领域特定可行性要求的解释，同时揭示了反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在生成透明、可行性感知的反事实解释方面具有巨大潜力，能够有效结合数据驱动预测与人类可理解的规则，为可解释AI提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [26] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一种用于联邦学习算法搜索的约束编码智能体工作流，在多个医疗和基准数据集上验证了其有效性，并系统分析了智能体生成候选方案的不同类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究涉及众多细微但关键的算法选择（如优化器变体、服务器聚合规则、本地训练计划等），这些选择人工探索成本高昂且难以公平比较，尤其当候选方案会改变FL训练或评估路径时。因此需要一个自动化、约束化的搜索框架来系统性地探索和评估这些算法选择。

Method: 提出AFR约束编码智能体工作流：智能体可提议并实现候选训练算法（包括服务器聚合规则、客户端更新计划、本地目标函数和模型变体），同时任务配置文件固定变异空间、计算预算、通信协议和最终模型评估。每个搜索活动记录候选分数、运行时间、编辑文件、产物和失败状态。在五个医疗跨孤岛FLamby任务和LEAF数据集上进行评估，采用五种子重复评估。

Result: 在四个FLamby任务和五个LEAF配置文件上实现了性能提升，但也暴露了种子敏感和搜索选择的失败案例。通过同预算对照实验发现：部分增益对应FL方案的改变，其他改进可通过固定空间标量控制恢复，或在重复/留出评估中失效。这些混合结果揭示了智能体生成候选方案可分为：可重复的FL机制、固定空间调优效应和单运行产物。

Conclusion: AFR展示了自动化FL算法搜索的可行性和复杂性：智能体生成的候选方案需要进行严格分类验证，区分真正可重复的FL机制改进、简单的超参数调优效应以及偶然的单次运行产物。这种分类分析是AFR框架的重要贡献，为未来FL算法自动化研究提供了方法论指导。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [27] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Wiola，一个从头构建的原创小型语言模型架构，包含五个创新组件：螺旋旋转位置编码、门控跨层注意力、自适应token合并、双流前馈网络和改进的归一化方法，在四个参数规模（120M-1.5B）上实现并与HuggingFace生态兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型架构（如GPT、LLaMA、Mistral、Falcon）存在结构相似性，缺乏从第一性原理出发的原创设计。研究旨在构建一个完全独立的架构，通过引入多种创新机制来提升位置编码表达能力、层间信息流动、计算效率和表示稳定性。

Method: 提出五个独立创新组件：(1)螺旋旋转位置编码(SRPE)在三维螺旋流形上嵌入token位置，融合绝对、相对和层次位置信号；(2)门控跨层注意力(GCLA)为每层提供对前两层压缩摘要的软跨注意力访问；(3)自适应token合并(ATM)在网络中层动态合并语义冗余的相邻token以降低注意力复杂度；(4)双流前馈网络(DSFF)用学习门控融合的双并行流替代传统MLP；(5)WiolaRMSNorm引入逐维度学习的偏移向量防止表示坍塌。

Result: 完成了完整的数学推导、架构框图、复杂度分析，并与GPT-2、LLaMA-2和Mistral进行系统对比。发布了四个规模版本（120M、360M、700M、1.5B参数），与HuggingFace Transformers生态系统完全兼容，22个架构单元测试全部通过。

Conclusion: Wiola成功构建了一个与现有模型家族无结构亲缘关系的原创语言模型架构，通过五个创新组件实现了位置编码、层间交互、计算效率和表示稳定性的改进，为语言模型架构设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [28] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agent4CS，一个用于总结大型代码库的多智能体框架。相比现有方法，Agent4CS在语义一致性上提升了8%，在关键词覆盖率上提升了38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码总结解决方案通常依赖于单一的语言模型或编程助手（如Claude Code），并将源代码视为扁平文本，未能充分利用代码库中丰富的相互依赖关系和层次结构信息。理解大型、复杂的代码库（尤其是那些具有混淆结构和文档不完整的代码库）仍然是一个重大挑战。

Method: 提出Agent4CS，这是一个以自底向上方式总结大型代码库的多智能体框架。该框架包含三个智能体：（1）总结智能体：专注于生成稳健的摘要；（2）关键词提取智能体：主动从子文件夹中识别关键信息；（3）质量保证智能体：迭代优化输出的可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上进行评估，Agent4CS在所有文件夹级别的语义一致性相比两个使用代码段的结构化提示基线平均提高了8%。此外，在真实世界数据集上的广泛评估显示，相比相同基线，归一化关键词覆盖率提高了38%。

Conclusion: Agent4CS通过多智能体协作和自底向上的总结方式，有效解决了现有代码总结方法在处理大型复杂代码库时的局限性，显著提升了语义一致性和关键信息覆盖率，证明了利用代码库层次结构和多智能体协作在代码理解任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [29] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规服务请求保持低成本基线路径，将复杂冲突请求路由到升级工作流，在关键后端写入操作前进行冲突感知通信和重新考虑，从而在零售和航空任务上提高可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服代理正从对话接口转向操作执行角色（如退款、取消、订单修改等），这带来了服务控制问题：企业需要在保持常规服务快速低摩擦的同时，防止在客户指令、政策约束、企业记录和后端写入相互作用的请求中出现操作错误。

Method: 提出难度路由的服务控制架构：1）轻量级路由器将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径；2）将操作耦合的会话路由到升级工作流；3）升级路径使用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑机制，在关键后端写入前集中审议和保护措施，而非在所有服务会话中统一应用额外控制。

Result: 在人工验证的零售和航空任务（τ²-bench）上评估：零售场景中，方法在有操作冲突的服务请求上一致提高可靠性；路由证据表明更强的控制指向冲突请求而非广泛应用于常规请求；对话和工具使用分析表明增益来自证据收集、写入分离和写入前重新考虑；案例证据显示升级工作流保留回退计划、绑定正确记录、序列化写入、分解多实体请求；航空结果将相同逻辑扩展到预订操作。

Conclusion: 该架构成功解决了自主客服代理的服务控制问题，通过智能路由实现了对复杂冲突请求的精准控制，在保持常规服务效率的同时提升了操作可靠性，证明了区分化服务控制策略的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [30] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CreativityNeuro方法，通过对比权重引导技术增强了大型语言模型的发散性思维能力，在多个创造力评估中取得了显著成果，且无需任何训练数据、重训练或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在回答开放性问题时倾向于生成相似的答案，这种现象被称为“人工群体思维效应”，但其发散性思维（创造力的关键要素）能力不足，因此需要一种方法来提高模型的创造力并减少模式崩塌问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为CreativityNeuro的无数据方法，利用对比权重引导技术来增强LLMs的发散性思维。该方法通过调整模型的权重来引导生成过程，而不需要任何行为数据、重训练或基于梯度的微调。

Result: 在Divergent Association Task（DAT）测试中，性能提升了高达14个人类百分点；在涉及720人的大规模人类评估中，在Alternative Uses Test（AUT）和Task Task上，原创性、惊喜度和创造力方面均有显著提升；在所有三项任务中都明显减少了模式崩塌的指标。此外，权重引导比激活引导在未见过任务上的泛化效果更好。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro提供了一种简单有效的方法来增强LLMs在创造性领域的表现，在不需任何训练数据、重训练或微调的情况下，成功提高了发散性思维并减少了模式崩塌问题。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>
