Result: 在受约束的底层平台配合约200行代码的文档CLI工具条件下，后门检测召回率从无约束无工具的54.5%提升至90.9%；底层平台和工具各自独立贡献性能提升。

Conclusion: 传统软件工程管理方法可有效迁移至AI编程代理的约束监督，在Python等默认保障较少的语言中底层监督效果最显著，原则可推广至Rust等语言。

Abstract: Coding agents are capable; human oversight is the bottleneck. Unconstrained agents introduce security risks, erode codebase scalability, and make human review increasingly costly. We argue that the same methods used for decades to manage large human engineering teams: access control, network policies, strict coding conventions enforced by tooling; transfer directly to coding agents, and are cheaper (in token) than recent agentic scaffolding. We sketch a start-to-end system on this principle, and report a controlled experiment in scalable oversight: a small reviewer (Gemma 4 e4b) inspects a Python codebase containing 11 inserted backdoors. Recall rises from 54.5% (unconstrained, no tools) to 90.9% (constrained substrate plus a ~200-LoC `docs` CLI), with substrate and tools contributing independently. We choose Python deliberately: substrate-level oversight gains are largest where the language gives the fewest guarantees by default; the principles extend to languages like Rust.

</details>


### [102] [Fast Multi-dimensional Refusal Subspaces via RFM-AGOP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02396)
*Thomas Winninger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于递归特征机（RFM）的高效算法，能在几秒内识别大型语言模型中的多维拒绝子空间，相比现有方法计算成本更低且在消融任务上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的用于提取大语言模型中复杂行为（如拒绝回答有害查询）多维子空间的方法计算成本昂贵，对于生成长推理链的推理模型来说更是不可行，亟需一种高效的子空间提取方法。

Method: 将递归特征机（RFM）算法进行改进，结合探针指导的初始化策略，用于高效识别多维拒绝子空间，在推理模型（Qwen 3）和非推理模型（Qwen 2.5）上进行验证。

Result: 该方法能够在几秒内识别出多维拒绝子空间，相比替代方法在消融任务上表现出更好的性能，同时具备更高的计算效率。

Conclusion: RFM方法有望成为现有大语言模型子空间提取方法的低成本、可扩展的补充方案，未来需要进一步研究不同方法所发现子空间之间的关系。

Abstract: Steering and monitoring activations in Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for both safety and interpretability. Early work assumed behaviours are encoded along single linear directions, but recent findings suggest complex behaviours, such as the refusal to answer harmful queries, live in multi-dimensional subspaces. However, existing methods for extracting these subspaces are computationally expensive, which becomes prohibitive on reasoning models who produce long reasoning traces. By adapting the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm -- which can be computed efficiently -- with a probe-informed initialization, we are able to identify the multi-dimensional refusal subspace in seconds, on reasoning (Qwen 3) and non-reasoning (Qwen 2.5) models. While RFM allows for faster subspace identification, it also showed better performances on the ablation task than its alternatives. More work is planned to better understand the relations between subspaces found by different methods. If confirmed, RFM could be a cheap and scalable complement to existing subspace-extraction methods in LLMs.

</details>


### [103] [Text-Driven 3D Indoor Scene Synthesis in Non-Manhattan Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02407)
*Xianhui Meng,Zirui Song,Yuchen Zhang,Li Zhang,Yongxuan Lv,Xiuying Chen,Kun Wang,Yan Luo,Kai Chen,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen,Jun Liu,Xiaoshuai Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SPG-Layout框架，通过统计先验和分层布局策略，解决了大语言模型在非曼哈顿环境中生成室内场景时物理合理性差的问题，在新构建的500个非曼哈顿环境基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的室内场景合成方法在非曼哈顿环境中表现不佳，难以捕捉合理的物体布局模式，主要原因是无法有效建模非正交空间关系，导致几何冲突高且物理保真度低。

Method: 提出SPG-Layout框架：1）利用物体分布的统计先验指导训练过程，增强环境理解和保真度；2）模仿人类设计流程，采用分层布局策略，优先放置大型物体，从而大幅减少布局冲突。

Result: 构建了包含500个多样化非曼哈顿环境的新基准；大量实验表明，SPG-Layout在曼哈顿和非曼哈顿环境中均持续且显著地优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SPG-Layout通过统计先验和分层布局策略的协同作用，实现了语义真实性和物理合理性的平衡优化，有效解决了复杂非曼哈顿环境中的室内场景生成难题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in 3D indoor synthesis for Manhattan environments. However, existing methods often fail to capture plausible object layout patterns in non-Manhattan settings, primarily because they struggle to model non-orthogonal spatial relationships, leading to high geometric violations and low physical fidelity. To address this challenge, we propose SPG-Layout, a novel text-driven framework designed to generate physically plausible indoor scenes within complex non-Manhattan environments. Specifically, we first utilize statistical priors of object distributions to guide the training process, enhancing environmental understanding and fidelity. Furthermore, mirroring human design workflows, we adopt a hierarchical layout strategy that prioritizes the placement of large objects, thereby substantially minimizing layout violations. By synergizing these components, SPG-Layout achieves a balanced optimization of semantic realism and physical plausibility. To evaluate performance in these complex settings, we constructed a new benchmark comprising 500 diverse non-Manhattan environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SPG-Layout consistently and significantly outperforms existing methods across both Manhattan and non-Manhattan environments. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [104] [Automated grading of Linux/bash examinations using large language models: a four-level cognitive taxonomy approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02432)
*Manuel Alonso-Carracedo,Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,Pedro Celard,Francisco J. Rodriguez-Martinez,Lorena Otero-Cerdeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了四种前沿大语言模型（GPT、Claude Opus、Gemini、GLM）在Linux/bash命令行考试评分中的表现，发现Gemini 3.0 Pro在评分标准引导下达到最高的人机一致性（ICC=0.888），并建立了基于认知复杂度的分类框架来预测AI评分适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机教育中招生规模扩大导致人工评分困难，而传统基于规则的自动评分器无法处理部分分数、等效解或语法变体。研究旨在探索大语言模型能否近似专家判断来评估短命令行响应，解决可扩展且可靠的考试评分挑战。

Method: 采用四层认知分类法（信息检索L1、基本文件操作L2、结构操作L3、高级系统管理L4），测试四种LLM在两种提示变体（最小基线和评分标准增强）下的表现。数据集包含1200名计算机工程二年级学生的真实响应，由三位专家独立评分作为基准。

Result: Gemini 3.0 Pro配合评分标准提示取得最高人机一致性（ICC(3,1)=0.888，MAE=0.10，Bland-Altman偏倚=-0.014）。一致性随分类层级提高而下降，高层次差距最大。所有模型中，评分标准质量的影响大于模型选择，结构化提示持续改善一致性。

Conclusion: 问题复杂度是LLM评分准确性的可靠预测因子。研究建立了基于分类法的原则性框架，用于判定哪些题目适合AI辅助评分、哪些需要人工审查，同时提供了可迁移的评估协议和提示模板。

Abstract: Scalable and reliable grading of command-line examinations remains a challenge in computing education, where rising enrolments make manual marking difficult and rule-based autograders cannot handle partial credit, equivalent solutions, or syntactic variation. This paper evaluates whether four frontier Large Language Models (GPT, Claude Opus, Gemini, and GLM) can approximate expert judgment when grading short Linux/bash command responses. The study adopts a four-level cognitive taxonomy that combines cognitive complexity and operational impact, ranging from information retrieval (L1) and basic file manipulation (L2) to structural operations (L3) and advanced system management (L4). The models were tested with two prompt variants, a minimal baseline and a rubric-enhanced version, on 1200 real responses from second-year Computer Engineering students independently graded by three expert instructors. Gemini~3.0 Pro with rubric-guided prompting achieved the highest human-AI agreement (ICC(3,1) = 0.888, MAE = 0.10, Bland-Altman bias = -0.014). Agreement declined consistently as taxonomy level increased, with the largest discrepancies at higher levels. Across all models, rubric quality had a larger effect than provider choice, with structured prompts consistently improving agreement. These results show that question complexity is a reliable predictor of the difficulty LLMs face in grading accurately, and they establish a principled, taxonomy-based framework for determining which questions are suitable for AI-assisted grading and which require human review, while also providing a transferable evaluation protocol and prompt templates.

</details>


### [105] [EvoPolicyGym: Evaluating Autonomous Policy Evolution in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02440)
*Zhilin Wang,Han Song,Runzhe Zhan,Jusen Du,Jiacheng Chen,Tianle Li,Qingyu Yin,Yulun Wu,Zhennan Shen,Tong Zhu,Yanshu Li,Guanjie Chen,Derek F. Wong,Yafu Li,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了自主策略进化（Autonomous Policy Evolution）评估框架和配套基准EvoPolicyGym，用于在固定交互预算下评估智能体通过反馈迭代改进可执行策略的能力。GPT-5.5在所有16个环境中表现最优，分析表明成功的策略进化依赖于发现任务适配机制和在有限反馈下精炼策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常将智能体通过反馈改进策略的过程简化为最终得分，或与开放式软件工程进度混淆，缺乏对策略迭代改进过程本身的受控评估。因此需要一个新的评估设置，专注于智能体如何在固定预算约束下反复编辑和改进可执行策略系统。

Method: 提出自主策略进化评估设置，其中Harness-Model智能体在固定交互预算下反复编辑可执行策略系统；构建EvoPolicyGym基准，基于紧凑交互式强化学习环境，评估智能体如何迭代改进探索策略；提供轨迹级别的诊断分析，区分智能体如何分配预算并将反馈转化为参数调优。

Result: GPT-5.5在EvoPolicyGym上获得最强综合排名得分，在全部16个环境中均达到前两名性能；轨迹级诊断分析揭示了强自主策略进化不仅依赖于孤立任务胜利，更依赖于发现任务适配机制和在有限反馈下精炼策略。

Conclusion: EvoPolicyGym为评估智能体的策略迭代改进能力提供了受控环境和细粒度诊断工具，证明了成功的自主策略进化需要智能体具备发现任务适配机制和在有限反馈约束下持续优化策略的能力，而非仅仅完成孤立任务。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly expected to improve executable policies through feedback, yet existing evaluations often collapse this process into a final score or confound it with open-ended software-engineering progress. We introduce Autonomous Policy Evolution, a controlled evaluation setting in which a harness-model agent repeatedly edits an executable policy system under a fixed interaction budget. We instantiate this setting in EvoPolicyGym, a benchmark built from compact interactive RL environments that evaluates how agents iteratively improve explored policies. On the EvoPolicyGym suite, GPT-5.5 achieves the strongest aggregate rank score and top-two performance on all 16 environments. Beyond leaderboard results, EvoPolicyGym also provides trajectory-level diagnostics that distinguish how agents allocate budget, convert feedback into parametric tuning. These analyses show that strong autonomous policy evolution depends not only on isolated task wins, but on discovering task-appropriate mechanisms and refining policies under bounded feedback.

</details>


### [106] [G-RRM: Guiding Symbolic Solvers with Recurrent Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02491)
*Timo Bertram,Sidhant Bhavnani,Richard Freinschlag,Erich Kobler,Andreas Mayr,Günter Klambauer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出G-RRM方法，将符号等变循环推理模型(SE-RRMs)与经典符号求解器结合以加速约束满足问题求解。实验表明，该方法在组合搜索空间广阔且求解器能动态修正分支选择时效果显著（如9×9数独上回溯加速33.3倍），但在运行时开销主导或求解器无法动态覆盖神经提示时则无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络求解器虽然展现出良好的泛化能力，但常产生不满足约束的无效解；而经典符号求解器虽能保证全局正确性，但在大规模组合问题上搜索效率较低。研究者希望结合两者优势，利用神经模型引导符号求解器以提高搜索效率。

Method: 提出"引导式循环推理模型"(G-RRM)：1) 训练符号等变循环推理模型(SE-RRM)作为神经求解器生成完整解提案；2) 将SE-RRM的输出作为分支提示注入经典符号求解器（包括回溯算法和SAT求解器如Glucose 4.1、CaDiCaL 3.0.0）；3) 系统性研究神经引导在何种条件下能提升符号求解器的搜索效率。

Result: 在数独问题上验证：1) 当条件满足时，9×9数独上回溯算法中位加速33.3倍，Glucose 4.1加速1.70倍(统计显著，p<0.001)；2) 在完美提示的25×25网格上Glucose 4.1仍保持1.17倍加速；3) CaDiCaL 3.0.0无显著加速（中位1.02倍），甚至出现轻微减速（均值0.90倍），因其运行时开销主导且无法动态覆盖神经提示；4) 识别出有效神经引导的两个必要条件：问题需具有广阔的组合搜索空间，且求解器架构需支持动态覆盖分支选择以从不完美提示中恢复。

Conclusion: 神经引导能否转化为实际加速取决于问题特性和求解器架构的匹配：当问题具有广阔组合搜索空间且求解器支持动态修正分支选择时，G-RRM能显著提升效率；反之，若求解器运行时开销主导或无法动态覆盖提示，则神经引导效果有限甚至有害。这为神经-符号混合方法的应用边界提供了清晰界定。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on SE-RRMs, a symbol-equivariant instantiation of RRMs that exhibits improved extrapolation to larger problem sizes. We propose a neuro-symbolic approach, ``Guiding with Recurrent Reasoning Models'' (G-RRM), which integrates SE-RRMs with symbolic solvers for constraint satisfaction problems. SE-RRMs act as neural solvers that generate full solution proposals and guide classical symbolic solvers, such as backtracking or SAT-based methods like Glucose 4.1 and CaDiCaL 3.0.0, that produce globally correct solutions. Centrally, we investigate when neural guidance with G-RRM improves the search efficiency of symbolic solvers. %
Our experiments show that the efficacy of G-RRM depends on two conditions: first, the problem instances must have an expansive combinatorial search space to expose potential gains, and second, the solver architecture must be capable of dynamically overwriting its branching choices to recover when neural hints are imperfect. When these conditions hold, guidance drives median conflict counts to zero and yields significant wall-clock speedups: on $9\times9$ Sudoku, where the SE-RRM correctly solves $91.1\%$ of instances, backtracking accelerates by $33.3\times$ and Glucose 4.1 by $1.70\times$ (median, $p<0.001$), with Glucose 4.1 retaining a $1.17\times$ speedup on perfect-hint $25\times25$ grids. In contrast, CaDiCaL 3.0.0, whose runtime is overhead-dominated and which always respects the injected branching hints rather than overwriting them, shows no significant speedup (median $1.02\times$, n.s.) and even a small significant mean slowdown ($0.90\times$) on $9\times9$. These results delineate the regimes in which neural guidance translates into practical speedups.

</details>


### [107] [ReContext: Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02509)
*Yanjun Zhao,Ruizhong Qiu,Tianxin Wei,Yuanchen Bei,Zhining Liu,Lingjie Chen,Ismini Lourentzou,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RECONTEXT，一种无需训练的长上下文推理方法，通过递归证据回放提升LLM的证据利用能力，在128K上下文长度下取得最优平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM虽支持长上下文窗口，但在实际应用中往往无法有效利用输入中已有的相关证据，存在上下文访问与有效利用之间的差距。

Method: 提出RECONTEXT方法：利用模型内部相关性信号构建查询条件化的证据池，在最终生成前进行递归证据回放，同时保留完整原始上下文，无需训练、外部记忆或上下文剪枝。

Result: 在8个长上下文数据集（128K上下文长度）上验证，RECONTEXT在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama3-8B三个主干模型上均取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: RECONTEXT成功弥合了上下文访问与有效利用之间的差距，为长上下文推理提供了一种高效的推理时解决方案，具有理论支撑和广泛的实用性。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning over long contexts has become a key requirement for deploying large language models (LLMs) in realistic applications. Although recent LLMs support increasingly long context windows, they often fail to use relevant evidence that is already present in the input, revealing a gap between context access and effective context utilization. In this work, we propose Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning (RECONTEXT), a training-free inference method for improving long-context reasoning. RECONTEXT uses model-internal relevance signals to construct a query-conditioned evidence pool and replays it before final generation while preserving the full original context. This recursive selection process separates evidence organization from answer generation without training, external memory, or context pruning. We also provide a theoretical analysis based on associative memory, which characterizes the context as a memory store, the question as a retrieval cue, attention as cue-trace association, and replay as trace reactivation. Experiments on eight long-context datasets with 128K context length show that RECONTEXT consistently improves evidence utilization across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama3-8B, achieving the best average rank on all three backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/ReContext.

</details>


### [108] [Online Safety Monitoring for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02510)
*Mona Schirmer,Metod Jazbec,Alexander Timans,Christian Naesseth,Maja Waldron,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种简单的实时监控方法，通过风险控制校准阈值，将外部模型的验证信号转换为报警决策，性能可媲美更复杂的监控方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM经过了安全对齐训练，但在部署时仍容易生成不安全的输出。因此，在线监控输出并在安全性无法保证时发出警报至关重要。

Method: 研究了一种简单的实时监控器，通过阈值化将外部模型的验证信号转换为报警决策，并通过风险控制来校准阈值。

Result: 在数学推理和红队测试数据集上的实验表明，这种简单的设计可与基于序贯假设检验的更高级监控器相媲美。

Conclusion: 简单的阈值化监控方法通过适当的风险控制校准，能够有效监控LLM的安全性，且性能不输于更复杂的方法。

Abstract: Despite alignment training, LLMs remain prone to generating unsafe outputs at deployment time. Monitoring outputs online and raising an alarm when safety can no longer be assumed is therefore critical. We study a simple real-time monitor that turns a verifier signal from an external model into an alarm decision by thresholding, with the threshold calibrated via risk control. In experiments on mathematical reasoning and red teaming datasets, we show that this simple design is competitive with more advanced monitors based on sequential hypothesis testing.

</details>


### [109] [Distributed Attacks in Persistent-State AI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02514)
*Josh Hills,Ida Caspary,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代代码生成场景下的新型攻击范式：恶意AI代理可通过跨多个PR分散攻击行为来规避监控，单一防御手段无法同时应对渐进式和集中式攻击，但结合跨PR状态追踪的监控器组合可显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代码代理自主性增强，代码库在会话间持续存在的特性创造了新的攻击面：恶意代理可将攻击分散到多个PR中，并在最佳时机触发载荷。现有防御研究缺乏对这种跨时间、跨PR的持久状态攻击动态的理解。

Method: 提出Iterative VibeCoding基准测试环境，让AI代理在持久代码库上通过序列PR构建软件，同时执行隐蔽副任务。对比渐进式攻击（跨PR分散副任务）与非渐进式攻击（集中在单个PR）。使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为攻击代理、GPT-4o作为监控器，在CLI工具和Flask Web服务两类共20个任务变体上评估。提出状态化链接追踪监控器，跟踪跨PR的可疑代码累积，并与四种监控器组合防御。

Result: 1）单一监控器无法同时防御两种攻击策略：最佳策略取决于监控器类型；2）高规避率（≥65%）在Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Kimi K2.5等多个模型后端上泛化，证实这是持久状态攻击面的特性；3）更先进的监控模型上规避率仍高，且渐进与非渐进式攻击的规避差距扩大；4）状态化链接追踪监控器在两类任务上检测渐进式攻击显著优于仅看历史累积的差异监控器；5）四监控器组合将渐进式攻击规避率从最弱标准差异监控下的93%降至47%。

Conclusion: 持久状态代码库创造了新型攻击面，攻击者可利用PR序列时序特性规避监控。防御者需组合多种监控策略：状态化跨PR追踪、轨迹监控等。未来AI代码系统部署需重视迭代开发场景下的安全防护，单一监控方案不足以应对多样化的攻击策略。

Abstract: As AI coding agents become more autonomous, they increasingly ship code iteratively, with the codebase persisting across sessions. This persistence creates a new attack surface: a misaligned or prompt-injected agent can distribute attacks across pull requests (PRs) and time its payload for the PR with the best natural cover. To study the resulting dynamics, we introduce Iterative VibeCoding, a setting for AI control, the study of safely deploying capable but potentially untrusted AI. In Iterative VibeCoding, a coding agent builds software over a sequence of PRs in a persistent codebase while pursuing a covert side task. Our benchmark includes two task families: CLI tools and Flask web services, across 20 total task variations. We use Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the attack agent and GPT-4o as the monitor. We compare gradual attacks, which distribute the side task across PRs, against non-gradual attacks concentrated in a single PR. No single monitor is robust to both: which strategy evades best (success while evading the monitor) depends on the monitor type, so a defender cannot close off both gradual and non-gradual attacks with any one monitor. High evasion (>= 65%) generalizes across model attack agent backends (Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5), confirming this is a property of the persistent-state attack surface rather than a single model's capability. Evasion also remains high across state-of-the-art monitor models and the gap between gradual and non-gradual evasion widens for more capable models. We introduce a stateful link-tracker monitor that tracks suspicious buildup across PRs. On both task families, it detects gradual attacks substantially better than diff monitors that merely see more accumulated history. Combining this stronger monitor with trajectory monitors in a four-monitor ensemble reduces gradual-attack evasion from 93% under the weakest standard diff monitor to 47%.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Field-Deployable RF Capture System for Indoor, Outdoor, and Foliage Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01368)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Vikas Ashok,Sachin Shetty,Peter B. Foytik,Jared Cochran,Jeff Bobrow,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种便携式、电池供电的射频采集系统，集成HackRF One SDR和树莓派5等组件，能够在现场环境中可靠记录带地理标记的IQ数据，用于频谱特性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的射频测量平台通常是实验室级别、成本高昂或依赖固定基础设施，限制了其在快速、分布式或长期现场测量活动中的实用性。因此需要一种可靠、可重复且适用于真实环境的射频测量解决方案。

Method: 开发了一套紧凑型电池供电射频采集系统，集成HackRF One软件定义无线电、树莓派5、GNSS接收器、稳压电源和高速固态存储。该平台能够以最高20 Msps速率记录连续IQ数据（SigMF格式），并为每段数据添加位置和时间元数据。在2.45 GHz频段进行了密集植被、城市户外和室内办公环境的现场实验。

Result: 植被环境测量结果接近噪声底（-76至-82 dBFS），频谱结构有限，符合强树冠衰减特征；城市环境显示30 dB动态范围内的多径活动、WiFi信道重叠和频繁的ISM频段干扰；室内环境显示WiFi信道占主导，相对户外有20-25 dB的建筑进入损耗，以及因结构反射导致的干扰底噪高出8-10 dB。系统持续写入吞吐量为75-85 MB/s，无样本丢失，GNSS同步时间小于1秒，定位精度达米级。

Conclusion: 便携式、低成本的SDR平台能够产生高保真、带地理标记的IQ数据集，可用于频谱特性分析、干扰分析、无线电环境测绘和环境感知无线研究。

Abstract: Reliable and reproducible radio-frequency (RF) measurements in real-world environments are essential for characterizing spectrum behavior across unlicensed ISM and WiFi bands, licensed mid-band allocations, and emerging next-generation wireless deployments. Existing measurement platforms are often laboratory-grade, cost-prohibitive, or dependent on fixed infrastructure, limiting their practicality for rapid, distributed, or long-duration field campaigns. This paper presents a compact, battery-powered RF capture system integrating a HackRF One software-defined radio, Raspberry Pi 5, GNSS receiver, regulated battery supply, and high-speed solid-state storage. The platform records continuous IQ data at up to 20 Msps in SigMF format with per-segment location and timing metadata for reproducible spectrum analysis. Field experiments at 2.45 GHz in dense foliage, urban outdoor, and indoor office environments reveal distinct propagation signatures. Foliage measurements remain near the noise floor at -76 to -82 dBFS with limited spectral structure, consistent with strong canopy attenuation. Urban measurements show multipath activity across a 30 dB dynamic range, overlapping WiFi channels, and frequent ISM-band interference. Indoor measurements show dominant WiFi channels, an estimated 20 to 25 dB building entry loss relative to outdoor conditions, and an 8 to 10 dB higher interference floor caused by structural reflections. The system sustained 75 to 85 MB/s write throughput with no dropped samples or buffer underruns, while GNSS synchronization remained below one second with meter-level positioning. These results show that a portable, cost-effective SDR platform can produce high-fidelity, geotagged IQ datasets for spectrum characterization, interference analysis, radio environment mapping, and environment-aware wireless research.

</details>


### [111] [Physically-Aware Preemptive Virtual Channels for Deadlock-Free AXI Networks-on-Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01430)
*Lorenzo Leone,Luca Colagrande,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对AXI4众核SoC中的NoC死锁问题，本文提出Preemptive VCs架构，在保持性能的同时节省高达76%的链路资源，路由器面积开销仅增加3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着众核SoC的扩展，片上网络需要维持高内存带宽并保持无死锁。在AXI4系统中，读写流量之间的协议级依赖会在网络端点产生循环等待，即使路由算法本身无死锁。分离这些流量类别可以避免依赖，但面临实现权衡：多平面NoC会复制宽物理链路并增加路由压力，而传统虚拟通道路由器则增加控制复杂性、面积和时序开销。

Method: 评估了四种无死锁AXI4流量类别分离方案：一个多平面基线方案和三个轻量级VC设计方案。提出Preemptive VCs，一种物理感知的架构设计，优化了现代宽链路NoC中的实现权衡。

Result: Preemptive VCs设计相比多平面方案，能够节省高达76%的链路资源，频率性能相当，路由器面积开销仅增加3%。

Conclusion: 对于现代宽链路NoC，Preemptive VCs架构在解决AXI4协议级死锁问题时，相比传统多平面方案显著降低了资源开销，是一种高效的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: As many-core Systems-on-Chip (SoCs) continue to scale, Networks-on-Chip (NoCs) must sustain increasingly high memory bandwidth while preserving deadlock freedom. In AXI4 systems, protocol-level dependencies between read and write traffic can create circular waits at the network endpoints, even when the routing algorithm itself is deadlock-free. Decoupling these traffic classes avoids such dependencies, but exposes a key implementation trade-off: multiplane NoCs duplicate wide physical links and increase routing pressure, whereas conventional Virtual Channel (VC) routers add substantial control complexity, area, and timing overhead. This work revisits this trade-off for modern wide-link NoCs. We evaluate four deadlock-free AXI4 traffic-class separation schemes: a multiplane baseline and three lightweight VC-based designs. Among these designs, we propose Preemptive VCs, a physically-aware architecture that can save up to 76% of link resources with comparable frequency and only 3% router area overhead relative to the multiplane design.

</details>


### [112] [MxGLUT: A Reconfigurable LUT-Centric Broadcast Dataflow Accelerator for Mixed-Precision GEMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01607)
*Weiyu Zhou,Chen Ding,Mingyuan Liu,Liangyu Gan,Yukun Feng,Hao Jia,Haoming Chu,Lirong Zheng,Ning Ma,Yuxiang Huan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MxGLUT，一种基于混合精度查找表（LUT）的可重构加速器，通过统一的LUT计算机制和可重构广播数据流，解决了大语言模型推理中权重仅量化的效率问题，实现了显著的面积和能耗节省，以及性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理在预填充和解码阶段效率低下，特别是在权重仅量化场景下（权重为低比特整数，激活值为FP8）。现有基于LUT的加速器主要针对FP8-INT4计算，仍依赖独立的浮点数据通路处理注意力GEMM操作，导致硬件冗余和混合精度执行不统一。此外，其静态数据流无法很好地适配预填充和解码阶段的不同特性。

Method: 提出MxGLUT加速器，基于混合精度LUT处理单元（MxLPEs），采用可重构LUT广播（RLB）数据流。核心创新包括：1）统一的LUT执行框架，将FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8 GEMM统一在单一LUT计算机制下，无需专用FP乘法器或额外FP数据通路；2）RLB数据流在预填充阶段局部化重量级部分和累加，在解码阶段利用权重重用。

Result: 在UMC 28nm CMOS工艺200MHz下综合，乘法器面积减少最多56.92%，功耗在FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8模式下分别降低最多77.07%和78.35%。加速器层面面积效率0.492 TFLOPS/mm²，能效11.58 TFLOPS/W。相比FP8-INT4-only基线，增加FP8-FP8支持仅带来2.57%面积效率和3.34%能效损失。在Llama模型上，预填充和解码阶段延迟加速比最高达2.16倍和1.49倍，归一化能耗降至0.44倍和0.71倍，困惑度增加最多仅1.70%。

Conclusion: MxGLUT通过统一的LUT计算机制和可重构数据流，有效解决了LLM推理中混合精度计算的效率问题。该方法在保持精度的同时显著降低了硬件开销和能耗，证明了LUT加速器在统一处理不同精度GEMM操作方面的优越性，为高效LLM推理硬件设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference suffers from growing inefficiency across the prefill and decode phases, especially under weight-only quantization, where activations remain in FP8 while weights are compressed to low-bit integers. Existing LUT-based accelerators mainly target FP8-INT4 computation and still rely on separate floating-point (FP) datapaths for attention GEMM operations, leading to redundant hardware and non-unified mixed-precision execution. Moreover, their static dataflows are poorly matched to the distinct prefill and decode phases. To address these challenges, we propose MxGLUT, a reconfigurable LUT-centric broadcast (RLB) dataflow accelerator built on mixed-precision LUT-based processing elements (MxLPEs). Guided by a unified LUT-based execution framework, MxGLUT organizes both FP8-INT4 and FP8-FP8 GEMMs under a single LUT-based compute mechanism without dedicated FP multipliers or additional FP datapaths, and further adopts the RLB dataflow that localizes heavy partial-sum accumulation during the prefill phase and exploits weight reuse in the decode phase. Synthesized in UMC $28\,\mathrm{nm}$ CMOS at $200~\mathrm{MHz}$, MxGLUT reduces multiplier area by up to $56.92\%$ and power by up to $77.07\%$ and $78.35\%$ in FP8-INT4 and FP8-FP8 modes, respectively. At the accelerator level, MxGLUT achieves an area efficiency of $0.492~\mathrm{TFLOPS/mm^2}$ and an energy efficiency of $11.58~\mathrm{TFLOPS/W}$, while adding native FP8-FP8 support incurs only $2.57\%$ and $3.34\%$ reductions in area and energy efficiency, respectively, relative to the FP8-INT4-only FIGLUT baseline. Across the Llama family, MxGLUT achieves up to $2.16\times$ and $1.49\times$ latency speedup, and reduces normalized energy to $0.44\times$ and $0.71\times$ in prefill and decode, respectively, with at most $1.70\%$ perplexity increase.

</details>


### [113] [3DLS: A 3D Logic-Stacked Architecture for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01617)
*Jaehun Lee,In-Jun Jung,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出3DLS，一种逻辑对逻辑的3D堆叠芯片架构，通过垂直互连传输KV缓存，避免与TP通信竞争，显著提升LLM服务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统的2D/2.5D芯片架构中，PD分离的LLM服务面临层间KV缓存传输与解码端TP通信共享同一横向D2D互连的问题，导致混合流量竞争，增加通信延迟，延长token生成间隔，降低端到端服务性能。

Method: 提出3DLS，一种逻辑对逻辑的3D堆叠芯片架构，将KV缓存传输通过垂直互连路由，而解码端的TP集合通信仍保留在横向D2D互连上，实现不同流量类别的物理隔离。

Result: 与共享平面基准相比，吞吐量提升高达1.49倍，端到端延迟降低60.2%；与负载感知优先级管理的平面基准相比，吞吐量提升高达1.17倍，端到端延迟降低31.4%。

Conclusion: 物理隔离是未来基于芯片的PD分离LLM服务系统的重要设计原则。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving increasingly combines prefill-decode (PD) disaggregation with tensor parallelism (TP) to support large models and long contexts. In conventional 2D/2.5D chiplet architectures, layer-wise prefill-to-decode KV-cache transfer decode-side TP collectives share the same lateral die-to-die (D2D) interconnect, creating mixed-traffic contention on the decode critical path. This contention increases communication latency, prolongs token generation intervals, and degrades end-to-end serving performance. We propose 3DLS, a logic-on-logic 3D-stacked chiplet architecture that separates traffic classes by routing KV-cache transfers through vertical interconnects while preserving decode-side TP collectives on the lateral D2D fabric. 3DLS achieves up to 1.49$\times$ throughput and 60.2\% lower end-to-end (E2E) latency over the shared-fabric planar baseline, and still achieves up to 1.17$\times$ throughput and 31.4\% lower E2E latency over a workload-aware priority-managed planar baseline. These results highlight that physical isolation is an important design principle for future chiplet-based PD-disaggregated LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [114] [Approximate Attention Weighting for Sustainable FPGA-Based Vision Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01798)
*Muhammad Usman,Shujaat Khan,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需BRAM的近似注意力权重单元，通过16段分段线性函数近似自然指数，在Xilinx Zynq-7020上实现了资源高效的ViT推理，精度损失仅0.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在边缘视觉检测领域具有广泛应用前景，但在小型FPGA上部署时，自注意力中的softmax阶段需要指数运算和归一化，硬件成本高昂。现有实现依赖CORDIC流水线或BRAM查找表，导致面积和功耗增加。

Method: 提出一种无BRAM的近似注意力权重单元，采用16段分段线性函数近似自然指数，完全使用分布式LUT实现。该方法保留预训练的注意力温度，避免了特定模型的重新校准。

Result: 在Xilinx Zynq-7020上实现的完整注意力行核心使用1444个LUT和77个DSP，无需BRAM。硬件精确仿真显示，在ViT系列模型上与精确softmax参考相比，top-1精度差异在0.20%绝对值以内。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了在资源受限的边缘AI平台上实现能效ViT推理的潜力，为FPGA部署Vision Transformers提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers have reshaped computer vision by using self-attention to capture global context across image regions. This makes them attractive for edge visual inspection and monitoring in applications such as renewable-energy infrastructure, industrial quality control, medical imaging, and autonomous-system sensing. However, deploying ViTs on small FPGAs remains challenging because the softmax stage in self-attention requires exponential evaluation and normalization, which are costly in hardware. Existing implementations often rely on CORDIC pipelines or BRAM-based look-up tables, increasing area and power consumption. This paper presents a BRAM-free approximate attention-weighting unit for FPGA-based ViT inference. The proposed design approximates the natural exponential in softmax using a 16-segment piecewise-linear function implemented entirely with distributed LUT fabric. Unlike base-2 approximations, the natural-exponential formulation preserves the pre-trained attention temperature and avoids model-specific recalibration. Implemented on a Xilinx Zynq-7020, the complete attention-row core uses 1444 LUTs, 77 DSPs, and no BRAM, while hardware-accurate emulation shows accuracy within a \(0.20\%\) absolute top-1 difference from the exact-softmax reference on ViT-family models. These results demonstrate the potential of the proposed core for energy-efficient ViT inference on resource-constrained edge-AI platforms.

</details>


### [115] [Probabilistic Memory for Trustworthy Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02465)
*Likai Pei,Jiahao Zheng,Xueji Zhao,Emilie Ye,Jianbo Liu,Hanqing Tao,Ming-Yen Lee,Ruiyang Qin,Yiyu Shi,Shimeng Yu,X. Sharon Hu,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出概率内存（p-MEM）统一原语，直接在内存带宽下采样，实现>1000 GSa/s/mm²的高斯随机数生成吞吐量，显著降低CPU/GPU系统开销，为可信边缘智能提供可扩展的硬件基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率计算在可信边缘智能中用于量化不确定性、增强鲁棒性、重构数据和保护隐私方面发挥重要作用，但其应用受到高斯随机数生成（GRNG）与计算之间存在数量级数据吞吐量差距以及指令开销的限制。

Method: 引入概率内存（p-MEM）统一原语，存储分布参数（如均值和标准差），在原生内存带宽下直接采样，确定性数据成为零方差的特例；使用布局验证的p-MEM模拟器全面探索器件选择、内存规格和技术节点。

Result: p-MEM可实现超过1000 GSa/s/mm²的GRNG吞吐量（包括内存阵列访问）；集成到CPU/GPU系统后，贝叶斯神经网络工作负载的指令计数减少高达2.19x/4.37x，采样延迟降低562x/3.45x，能耗减少295.5x/3.53x。

Conclusion: p-MEM为可信概率AI提供了可扩展的硬件基础，有效解决了概率计算中的吞吐量瓶颈和指令开销问题。

Abstract: Probabilistic computation plays an important role in trustworthy edge intelligence to quantify uncertainty, enhance robustness, reconstruct data, and protect privacy, but its adoption is limited by the orders-of-magnitude data throughput gap between Gaussian random number generation (GRNG) and computation, as well as instruction overhead. This paper introduces probabilistic memory (p-MEM), a unified memory primitive that stores distribution parameters, such as mean and standard deviation, and samples directly at the native memory bandwidth, where deterministic data becomes the zero-variance special case. Using a layout-validated p-MEM simulator, we comprehensively explore device choices, memory specifications, and technology nodes, showing that p-MEM can achieve more than 1000 GSa/s/mm^2 GRNG throughput, including memory-array access. Integrated into CPU/GPU systems, p-MEM reduces instruction count by up to 2.19x/4.37x, sampling latency by 562x/3.45x, and energy by 295.5x/3.53x for Bayesian neural network workloads, providing a scalable hardware substrate for trustworthy probabilistic AI.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出M-QCDNet模型，通过Q矩阵结构先验和L2惩罚项，融合认知诊断模型的可解释性与神经网络的灵活性，在保持认知理论一致性的同时实现精准诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型虽具可解释性但表达能力有限，而深度神经网络虽灵活但缺乏结构可解释性，亟需将两者优势结合以提供既符合认知理论又具有强预测能力的诊断工具。

Method: 提出M-QCDNet多层神经网络架构：1)利用Q矩阵作为结构先验建模题目-技能关系，确保潜在掌握状态与认知理论一致；2)设计包含L2惩罚项的损失函数，惩罚与Q矩阵不匹配的技能激活，平衡预测性能与结构对齐；3)开发基于对齐的可解释性评估指标，量化预测技能激活与题目技能的对应程度。

Result: M-QCDNet成功实现了认知诊断模型的结构可解释性与神经网络灵活性的融合，既保证了潜在掌握状态的可解释性和认知理论一致性，又获得了优异的预测性能，评估指标验证了其技能激活预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将诊断有效性嵌入模型设计，M-QCDNet在心理测量透明度与神经灵活性之间架起桥梁，为教室实践提供早期学习困难检测和基于掌握的干预支持，推动了认知诊断领域中可解释、公平、可操作AI的发展。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [117] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于跨被试EEG压力检测的黎曼流形注意力网络，通过频率特定的空间协方差建模和切片级注意力机制，在三个数据集上实现了优于现有方法的性能（最高82.78%准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试EEG压力检测面临挑战，因为判别性压力相关模式既具有被试依赖性又具有频率特异性。传统黎曼方法主要在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了高级认知状态解码至关重要的神经振荡，而标准时间标记化常常破坏切片间的时间连贯性。

Method: 提出I2RMAN方法：1）在每个频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道几何和频率特定判别线索；2）引入频率聚类聚合选择信息性频谱成分并减少冗余；3）设计切片内外注意力模块自适应整合局部切片级频谱动态和全局时间上下文。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，I2RMAN持续优于五个最先进的基线方法，实现了高达82.78%的平衡准确率，同时保持高效性，仅有1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs。

Conclusion: 通过频率特定的黎曼流形建模和自适应注意力机制，该方法有效解决了跨被试EEG压力检测中的关键挑战，在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [118] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究PBE系统在对抗性攻击下的脆弱性，发现低边际任务存在被传统噪声评估忽略的对抗鲁棒性维度；提出的防御方法VPA仅在任务存在投票边际时有效，而在现实任务中往往失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE鲁棒性研究主要关注随机噪声（如拼写错误），但忽略了对抗性攻击场景——攻击者看到合成器后故意选择最易被破坏的示例进行攻击。本文旨在揭示这一被忽视的失败模式。

Method: 1) 形式化有限PBE版本空间上的固定集最坏情况损坏问题；2) 为字符串转换DSL实现精确（有界池内）和启发式损坏搜索攻击；3) 提出版本空间分区聚合(VPA)防御：在不相交示例组上合成并通过语义签名投票。

Result: 对抗攻击极其有效：一个精心设计的编辑可在预算1内翻转全部8个spike任务，而200次拼写错误/DSL池/随机对照成功率仅10.3%/11.0%/16.7%；VPA防御在存在投票边际时有效（如生成的边际-1任务），但在公共SyGuS数据上因投票边际接近1而被自适应攻击者将准确率降至零。

Conclusion: 低边际PBE任务存在对抗性脆弱性维度，这是随机噪声评估无法捕捉的；语义分区聚合防御仅在有足够投票边际时有效，而现实任务往往缺乏这一条件，揭示了对立设置的固有局限性。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [119] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于ECG识别，通过引入PRQST关键点特征点作为领域知识，使用双流有向图建模ECG周期内和周期间的关系，在First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition数据集上达到88.1%的平均F1分数，稀有类别平均F1分数为76.3%，均超越现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人工智能技术广泛应用，但AI模型的可解释性仍是挑战，尤其在心电图（ECG）识别等医疗专业领域。现有方法主要依赖端到端卷积神经网络，缺乏对领域专业知识的整合，限制了模型的可解释性和性能表现。

Method: 提出一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络方法：（1）将ECG解读中至关重要的PRQST关键点特征作为领域知识融入模型；（2）采用双流有向图结构，空间有向图捕获关键点之间的位置关系，时间有向图描述扩展ECG序列中相邻周期之间的时间依赖关系，从而同时建模ECG周期内和周期间的关系。

Result: 在First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition数据集上进行九分类ECG识别实验，整体平均F1分数达到88.1%，稀有类别平均F1分数为76.3%，两项指标均超越当前最先进模型。领域知识的引入显著提升了检测性能，尤其对稀有类别的识别效果提升明显。

Conclusion: 研究证明了将领域知识整合到深度学习模型中的有效性，基于图卷积网络的方法不仅提升了ECG识别性能，特别是稀有类别的识别能力，同时增强了模型的可解释性，为医疗AI模型的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [120] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了多探针网格算法在近似最近邻搜索中的性能，发现其在GloVe嵌入数据集上具有更优的维度扩展性、接近线性的查询扩展性以及更低的索引成本，适用于重建频繁或高维度场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的近似最近邻搜索扩展性分析研究中缺乏对基于网格方法的系统性评估；同时，鉴于近期研究将自注意力形式化为ANN操作，分析ANN算法在数据规模N和维度d上的扩展特性对于指导高效Transformer架构的成本分析具有重要意义。

Method: 对多探针网格算法进行系统性实验表征，重点分析其在不同数据集规模N和维度d下的性能表现，并与现有的图、树、划分等主流ANN方法进行对比。

Result: 在GloVe嵌入数据集上发现了一个此前未报道的维度扩展性交叉点：多探针网格搜索维持近似恒定的维度扩展指数，而其他方法吞吐量随维度升高而下降；同时具备接近线性的N扩展性和比竞争方法更低的索引成本。

Conclusion: 基于网格的方法（如多探针网格）在索引成本和维度鲁棒性主导性能的场景下（如重建频繁或高维设置）具有竞争力；其N和d扩展特性可为高效Transformer架构的成本分析提供指导。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [121] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了IonSense-QKG框架，为锂离子电池数据集添加量子相关性元数据，并通过量子就绪度评分来筛选适用于混合量子-经典机器学习工作流的公开数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公开锂离子电池数据集在化学成分、模态、规模、标签质量等方面存在显著差异，这些差异直接影响数据集是否适用于近期混合量子-经典机器学习工作流，缺乏系统的数据集筛选标准。

Method: 基于EV-Battery-IonSense索引，构建量子就绪元数据框架，为数据集记录添加任务类型、传感模态、化学成分、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理要求、候选量子编码、预估量子比特范围和NISQ可行性等元数据；引入透明的量子就绪度评分对数据集进行排序，支持基于查询的数据集发现。

Result: 框架支持通过查询方式识别适合紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测以及未来电池健康基准测试的数据集；发布了包含元数据表、评分脚本、鲁棒性检查、链接检查工具和SQL风格查询示例的完整工件。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，为以数据为中心的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础，为未来混合量子-经典电池基准测试提供数据集筛选启发式方法。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [122] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合稀疏随机投影降维和多项式逻辑回归分类的DNA甲基化CNS肿瘤分类新方法，在91类分类任务上达到86%准确率，在分类族水平达到93%准确率，分别比现有最佳方法提高约4和5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DNA甲基化谱分析已成为CNS肿瘤分类的有力工具，但在跨队列可迁移性、方法学正确性和稳健的多类评估方面仍存在重要挑战。现有参考分类器在临床评估队列上的准确率有限，需要更准确可靠的分类方法来支持临床诊断和治疗决策。

Method: 提出一种方法学严谨的机器学习方法，结合稀疏随机投影进行维度降维，使用多项式逻辑回归进行分类。在与广泛使用的参考分类器相同的实验设置下进行评估，包括2,801样本的参考队列（分层3折交叉验证）和1,104样本的独立临床评估队列。

Result: 在参考队列上通过分层3折交叉验证达到96%平均准确率；在独立临床评估队列上，91类水平准确率达86%，甲基化分类族水平准确率达93%。相比现有最佳方法的82%（类水平）和88%（族水平）准确率，分别获得约4和5个百分点的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 基于更强机器学习方法论实践的模型在各项评估设置中持续优于先前的最先进方法，能够实质性提高CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性。在诊断环境中，5个百分点的正确肿瘤分类提升可直接影响癌症亚型分配，进而影响治疗选择和下游临床决策制定，具有重要的临床意义。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [123] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文献（书籍）致力于构建一个统一的、面向证明的现代深度学习理论框架，从经典的近似、优化和泛化基础，延伸至过参数化、鲁棒性、生成模型、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐以及涌现等前沿主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的发展已超越了单一的数学解释。为了理解现代深度学习理论的全貌，需要从孤立的结论转向连贯的研究叙事，系统地组织广泛的文献，为研究人员、研究生和具备数学背景的从业者提供严谨的理论地图。

Method: 采用统一、面向证明的叙述方式，将理论按照其控制的对象、有效性的假设条件以及无法解释的现象三个维度进行组织与审视。从经典基础（近似、优化、泛化）出发，逐步构建至现代机制（过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐、涌现），形成连贯的理论叙事。

Result: 成功勾勒出现代深度学习理论的严谨版图：揭示了理论既强大又不完全，且日益聚焦于规模、数据、架构与训练如何催生学习机制这一核心问题。为读者提供了一个系统的框架，用以审视各项理论的有效范围与局限。

Conclusion: 现代深度学习理论的核心挑战在于理解学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现。本文献为领域提供了一个强有力的理论地图，同时承认理论尚不完整，为未来的研究方向指明了路径。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [124] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了NightVision攻击方法，即使仅通过受限的黑盒API访问（只能获取单个解码token的对数概率），仍能成功恢复LLM的隐藏维度、深度和参数量等架构细节，平均相对误差分别为23%、53%，证明了当前LLM API的限制不足以完全掩盖模型架构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM提供商为防止架构细节泄露，已将API限制为仅暴露单个token的对数概率并移除了logit偏置功能。本文旨在探究在如此受限的API条件下，是否仍能恢复LLM的架构参数，以评估当前API限制措施的有效性。

Method: 提出NightVision攻击方法，包含两个核心技术：1) 通用集合提示技术（common set prompting），通过多个提示为相同的输出token集合暴露对数概率，利用谱分析推断隐藏维度；2) 结合端到端首个token时间（TTFT）测量和估计的隐藏维度来推断模型深度和参数量。

Result: 在32个开源LLM上的实验表明：隐藏维度恢复的平均相对误差为23%（MoE模型仅9%）；对于超过30亿参数的模型，深度和参数量的恢复误差在53%以内。消融实验进一步验证了准确率随token预算和模型特性的变化规律。

Conclusion: 当前LLM API的限制措施仍不足以完全掩盖底层模型的架构细节，存在信息泄露风险。这一发现对LLM提供商的API设计和安全策略具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [125] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于多模态数据的铁路道口安全评估系统，通过结合图像和事故报告等结构化数据，实现了高风险和低风险道口的分类（F1=0.757）以及与FRA标准一致的安全评分预测（RMSE=0.078，相关性=0.492）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路道口安全评估是一个重要但复杂的问题。研究者希望探索是否可以通过视觉线索来鲁棒地估计铁路道口的安全性，并研究引入事故历史等结构化数据是否能提升模型性能，最终构建一个能够整合多模态数据并提供与专家意见和联邦铁路管理局（FRA）安全评分一致的AI评估系统。

Method: 提出了一个概念验证管道，该管道能够处理多模态数据（图像和结构化事故报告数据），采用路由微调的紧凑型视觉语言模型（VLM）架构。研究涵盖了从数据准备到不同学习范式的完整流程，针对管道各部分的关键研究挑战进行了探索和解决。

Result: 系统在风险分类任务上实现了0.757的宏F1分数（区分高风险和低风险道口）；在FRA安全评分估计任务上达到了0.078的RMSE和0.492的相关性；生成的定性结果与领域专家评估相一致。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了通过多模态数据融合进行铁路道口安全评估的可行性，提出的基于路由微调紧凑VLM的管道能够有效识别风险等级并预测安全评分，且评估结果与专家意见和官方标准保持一致，为AI辅助铁路安全评估提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [126] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了基于Transformer的序列处理（尤其是电子健康记录数据）中数值编码的离散、连续和混合策略。研究发现，显式建模值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感任务上表现最佳，而基于分箱的混合标记方法提供了更鲁棒和通用的解决方案，最优分箱数与数据集大小呈幂律关系。临床实践中，鲁棒性和可部署性通常比最大数值精度更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）数据包含大量数值型实验室检验结果，如何在Transformer模型中有效编码这些数值信息是一个重要但研究不足的问题。现有编码策略缺乏系统性比较，且数值精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性之间存在权衡关系，需要为实践提供明确的指导方案。

Method: 研究系统地比较了三种数值编码策略：离散编码、连续编码和混合编码。通过在真实EHR数据中嵌入合成算术任务来评估数值精度，同时使用真实临床预测任务验证实用性。研究分析了不同方法在数值精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性方面的表现，并探索了最优分箱数量与数据集大小的关系。

Result: 显式建模值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感的算术任务中表现最佳（当架构约束允许时）。基于分箱的混合标记方法提供了更鲁棒和广泛适用的替代方案，最优分箱数遵循数据集大小的经验幂律关系。模型在所有任务中均表现出可靠的"足够好"数值计算能力而非精确算术。实验室数值的纳入对临床任务的改进效果因任务而异。

Conclusion: 在临床实践中，鲁棒性和可部署性往往比最大数值精度更重要。研究建议采用基于分箱的混合标记方法作为实用的默认策略。这为EHR数据中的数值编码提供了实践指导，强调了实用性与精度之间的平衡。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [127] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeuroBridge框架，通过结合大规模自监督MRI预训练与临床指导的多任务学习（海马体分割、萎缩分类、重建），显著提升了阿尔茨海默病及相关认知障碍的MRI诊断性能，在跨队列迁移和机会性筛查中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MRI准确识别阿尔茨海默病（AD）、轻度认知障碍（MCI）及相关痴呆仍然具有挑战性，因为疾病相关的结构变化通常微妙且具有异质性。

Method: NeuroBridge整合了大规模自监督MRI预训练与多任务目标（海马体分割、海马体萎缩分类、重建），随后进行门控融合微调。研究在ADNI和OASIS队列上进行了评估，包括跨队列迁移、基于概率的分析和机会性筛查测试。

Result: NeuroBridge在所有评估的分类任务中均达到最佳性能，在ADNI数据集上AD与认知正常对照组分类准确率达88.17%，在OASIS上达82.78%。在MCI相关和混合诊断场景中提升最为显著。该框架展示了强大的跨队列泛化能力，预测类别概率与准确率之间存在系统性关联，并验证了基于概率的机会性筛查的可行性。

Conclusion: 临床指导的多任务表示学习在神经退行性MRI诊断方面优于传统单任务方法。NeuroBridge为痴呆症评估和基于MRI的机会性筛查提供了一个稳健且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [128] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SpinGTP方法，通过推广到自旋加权球谐函数(SWSH)克服GTP的不完整性，在保持渐近效率的同时恢复缺失的反对称相互作用，实现与完整CGTP相当的精度，在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何的任务中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E(3)等变网络在3D原子系统建模中很有前景，但其可扩展性受到Clebsch-Gordan张量积(CGTP)的O(L^6)复杂度限制。最近提出的Gaunt张量积(GTP)虽然降低了复杂度，但无法捕获反对称路径，导致表达能力不完整。

Method: 提出SpinGTP方法，将标量函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数(SWSH)，利用SWSH的代数性质恢复缺失的反对称相互作用，同时保持GTP的渐近效率。该方法还允许更具表现力的等变基，自然地考虑张量积的奇宇称分量。

Result: 在Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF和OC20等多个基准测试中，SpinGTP达到与完整CGTP相当的精度。通过显式捕获反对称路径，SpinGTP在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何的任务中表现优越。

Conclusion: 本工作为大规模3D原子系统模拟中的高阶等变性提供了一条完整、可扩展且数学上严谨的路径，成功解决了GTP的表达能力不完整问题，同时保持了计算效率。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [129] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对比分析了四种执行环境（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes容器和云虚拟机）在编码智能体强化学习中的性能差异，发现冷启动延迟存在高达110倍的差异，在百万轨迹模拟中预计有1.8倍的工作时间差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体强化学习研究将执行基础设施视为后台实现细节而忽略，但在大规模交互式软件rollout中，测量基础设施开销可以揭示强化学习后训练的实际效率提升机会，因为每个rollout的微小节省会在规模上累积。

Method: 对四种执行环境进行全面对比研究：单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器和云虚拟机，测量关键性能指标包括冷启动延迟和投影工作时间等。

Result: 发现冷启动延迟存在高达110倍的差异，对于一百万个150步轨迹的模拟，投影工作时间存在1.8倍的差异范围。

Conclusion: 未来的编码智能体强化学习系统应将执行环境优化作为训练系统本身的组成部分，而不仅仅是部署管道的基础设施，以获得更好的整体性能。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [130] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了条件推断森林（CIF）作为一种top-k特征排序方法的有效性。CIF在分类任务中排名第4（共17种方法），在回归任务中排名第3（共18种方法）。自适应停止和阈值搜索策略对运行时间影响最大，关闭自适应停止和精确阈值搜索分别增加4.0-8.4倍和1.9-10.8倍的拟合时间，但对下游预测分数影响甚微（最多0.011）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件推断树（CIT）和条件推断森林（CIF）通过在选择分裂阈值前对特征进行测试来减少分裂选择偏差，但重复的置换测试和阈值搜索使这些方法计算成本高昂。本文旨在研究CIT和CIF作为top-k特征排序方法在下游预测任务中的有效性，并分析其计算效率与性能权衡。

Method: 研究采用了三种方法：(1) 在22个真实数据集上比较17种分类方法，在8个数据集上比较18种回归方法；(2) 运行时间消融实验，分析自适应停止、阈值搜索数量等因素对运行时间的影响；(3) 合成特征恢复实验，模拟稀疏高维场景下的特征选择性能。在统计推断方面，证明了在固定节点处，当特征和置换预算不依赖节点响应时，Bonferroni校正的+1 Monte Carlo置换p值能在完全置换原假设下控制节点拒绝率。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) CIF在分类任务中排名第4（共17种方法，22个数据集），在回归任务中排名第3（共18种方法，8个数据集）；(2) 自适应停止和阈值搜索数量对运行时间影响最大：关闭自适应停止增加4.0-8.4倍拟合时间，使用精确阈值搜索增加1.9-10.8倍拟合时间，而下游分数变化最多仅0.011；(3) 稀疏高维模拟显示森林特征采样可能导致信息性特征被排除在许多分裂决策之外。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将CIF作为下游预测基准中的top-k特征排序方法。虽然CIF计算成本较高，但通过自适应停止和近似阈值搜索可以在保持预测性能的同时显著降低计算开销。Bonferroni校正的置换检验提供了有效的统计控制。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [131] [DeadPool: Resilient LLM Training with Hot-Swapping via Zero-Overhead Checkpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01646)
*Haotian Xie,Junlin Chen,Mingkai Zheng,Lishan Yang,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DeadPool，一种用于大规模LLM训练的容错机制，通过热插拔和内存检查点技术，实现了零开销的正常执行和极低恢复延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模LLM训练容错机制存在权衡困境：要么在无故障执行期间引入显著开销，要么面临漫长的恢复延迟，特别是在少数计算节点发生永久性故障的场景下。

Method: 提出了DeadPool系统，包含两个核心创新：1) 非关键路径上的内存检查点机制用于空间冗余；2) 运行时通信器重建协议，支持用备用节点热插拔替换故障节点。该系统将内存检查点与计算重叠，实现零开销。

Result: 在多达512张NVIDIA A100 GPU和650亿参数的LLM上评估，结果显示零检查点开销，热插拔恢复在40秒内完成。

Conclusion: DeadPool成功同时实现了零开销的无故障执行和极低的恢复成本，为大规模LLM训练提供了高效的容错解决方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art large language model (LLM) training takes tens of thousands of graphics processing units (GPUs) for months and encounters failures across the software and hardware stack. Existing fault-tolerance mechanisms either impose non-trivial overhead during failure-free execution or suffer from prolonged recovery latency, particularly under scenarios where a small subset of compute nodes experience permanent failures. %The tradeoff between failure-free overhead and recovery latency forms a space forms a Pareto frontier We present DeadPool to simultaneously address both optimization objectives. DeadPool incorporates a fault-tolerance mechanism that restores LLM training via hot-swapping, namely by replacing failed nodes with spare nodes without terminating the complete job. The hot-swapping of DeadPool is enabled by two ideas: First, it exploits an off-critical-path in-memory checkpointing mechanism for spatial redundancy. Second, it introduces a communicator reconstruction protocol that replaces failed nodes with spare nodes at runtime. DeadPool efficiently overlaps the in-memory checkpointing with computation, thus introducing zero overhead during error-free execution. Upon permanent node failures, DeadPool can rebuild memory states with minimal recomputation by leveraging in-memory checkpoints. We evaluate DeadPool across scales (up to 512 NVIDIA A100 GPUs) and LLMs (up to 65B parameters), and observe zero checkpoint overhead with hot-swapping recovery completing in under 40 seconds. These results show that DeadPool simultaneously achieves both zero-overhead error-free execution and extremely low recovery cost.

</details>


### [132] [On the Utility and Factual Reliability of Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Models in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01444)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合专家模型（MoE）在特定领域（生物医学）剪枝对模型实用性和事实可靠性的影响，发现适度剪枝可保持领域内实用性，但跨领域表现急剧下降，且在极端剪枝下幻觉风险增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE模型专家剪枝研究主要关注基准测试的实用性，而忽略了剪枝对事实可靠性的影响，特别是在生物医学等高风险领域。为了在资源受限环境中安全部署MoE模型，需要深入理解剪枝对实用性和可靠性的双重影响。

Method: 评估了四种MoE模型、六种剪枝方法和多种剪枝比例，在领域内（生物医学）和跨领域设置下，针对生成和分类任务进行全面分析，系统地考察剪枝对实用性和可靠性的影响。

Result: 适度剪枝可以在不立即降低可靠性的情况下保持领域内实用性，但在极端剪枝比例下幻觉风险增加。当转移到通用领域时，实用性和可靠性都迅速下降。安全的压缩高度依赖于任务和领域。

Conclusion: 仅基于实用性评估剪枝后的MoE模型对于高风险部署是不充分的，必须同时评估可靠性。安全的MoE模型压缩需要考虑具体任务和领域的特性，这对于在资源受限环境中部署AI系统具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [133] [SCAPE: Accurate and Efficient LLM Training with Extreme Sparse Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01678)
*Mingkai Zheng,Junlin Chen,Haotian Xie,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCAPE，一种面向LLM分布式训练的通信高效优化器，通过利用AdamS一阶矩的稳定性实现高达99%的稀疏化，在保持模型质量的同时将端到端预训练时间减少高达43.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型规模和系统规模的增长，梯度同步和参数重构的通信开销已成为分布式预训练的主要瓶颈。现有方法中，直接稀疏化原始梯度在高稀疏度下对Adam类优化器不稳定，而量化方法的压缩效果受限于比特位宽且引入额外运行时开销。

Method: SCAPE基于AdamS一阶矩统计量生成掩码而非原始梯度，将掩码生成工作分区以适配优化器分片，并将掩码使用延迟一步以实现掩码同步与计算重叠；同时通过单一同步稀疏缓冲区重构二阶矩更新所需量，避免额外的集合通信操作。

Result: 在32块NVIDIA GH200 GPU上的实验表明：SCAPE在90%和99%稀疏度下均能保持训练稳定性、验证损失和下游任务准确率；对于Llama-500M，端到端预训练时间减少高达43.3%；对于Llama-1.8B，每步训练速度提升达3.26倍。

Conclusion: SCAPE成功验证了利用AdamS一阶矩稳定性实现激进稀疏化的可行性，在显著降低通信开销的同时保持与稠密AdamW和AdamS相当的模型质量，为大规模LLM分布式训练提供了一种高效的通信优化方案。

Abstract: Communication increasingly dominates the cost of Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training, especially under data-parallel and sharded training schemes, where gradient synchronization and parameter reconstruction overhead increase with model size and system scale. Existing communication-reduction methods either sparsify raw gradients, which can be unstable for modern Adam-style optimizers at high sparsity, or quantize communication, whose savings are fundamentally bounded by bit width and often incur additional runtime overhead. We present SCAPE, a communication-efficient distributed optimizer for LLM training that exploits the stability of AdamS's first-moment to enable aggressive sparsification without loss of LLM quality. Instead of constructing masks from raw gradients, SCAPE derives them from first-moment-based statistics, partitions mask generation across workers to align with optimizer sharding, and delays mask usage by one step so that mask synchronization can overlap with computation. SCAPE also reconstructs the quantities required for second-moment updates from a single synchronized sparse buffer, avoiding an additional collective. We implement SCAPE in Megatron-LM and evaluate its convergence by pre-training GPT-345M on OpenWebText and Llama-500M on SlimPajama-6B using 32 NVIDIA GH200 GPUs on TACC Vista. In both models, SCAPE preserves training stability, validation loss, and downstream task accuracy under 90\% and 99\% sparsity. For Llama-500M, SCAPE reduces end-to-end pre-training wall-clock time by up to 43.3\% while maintaining model quality comparable to dense AdamW and AdamS. For Llama-1.8B, SCAPE achieves up to 3.26$\times$ speedup per step compared to dense AdamS.

</details>


### [134] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出几何感知R结构Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（GRS-KAN），将R函数与KAN框架集成，通过可微分逻辑运算显式编码几何约束，在测试基准上测试RMSE最高降低67%，同时提升模型可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络难以显式表示不连续性、可行区域和隐式几何边界，且缺乏将已知几何或逻辑约束融入模型的有效机制，限制了模型在需要几何先验知识任务上的性能和可解释性。

Method: 将V.L.Rvachev的R函数集成到Kolmogorov-Arnold网络框架中，构建混合神经架构。KAN分支学习平滑非线性结构，可微分R函数解析编码几何或逻辑约束。通过R-合取和R-析取实现可微分逻辑运算，将复杂几何支撑直接纳入回归模型。提出加性、乘性和不可知分支加权三种GRS-KAN变体。

Result: 在涉及圆形和矩形支撑的不连续性回归问题上进行验证。几何感知GRS-KAN模型相比标准KAN显著提升预测精度和边界定位能力，测试RMSE最高降低67%。不可知变体能够自动判断几何先验对特定学习任务是否有益。

Conclusion: 显式几何编码可有效提升神经网络的预测精度和可解释性。GRS-KAN框架成功实现了几何约束与数据驱动学习的有机融合，为需要几何先验的建模问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [135] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ember，一种针对语言模型嵌入层和LM-head的轻量级优化器，仅需O(V+D) VRAM（相比Adam的O(2VD)），通过利用嵌入接口独特的梯度几何特性，在仅使用KB级别优化器状态的情况下，在监督微调、强化学习和预训练任务上改善了Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在离散符号上学习连续程序，其嵌入表和LM-head作为离散与连续之间的读写接口。研究发现该接口具有与密集隐藏权重不同的梯度几何特性，这可以被利用来优化训练过程，同时大幅降低内存开销，避免了分片token表优化器状态的需求。

Method: 提出Ember优化器，专门针对嵌入矩阵和LM-head设计；利用O(V+D)的VRAM复杂度（V为词表大小，D为嵌入维度）；基于嵌入接口梯度几何的独特性设计优化策略；提供了与现有ZeRO/FSDP分布式设置兼容的开源实现。

Result: 实验证明Ember在批大小和参数规模上具有良好的扩展性；发现token的优化轨迹可以用简单的1D射线很好地描述，这与神经网络参数在高度非凸景观中导航的普遍认知相悖；揭示了Transformer训练中优化器空间狭窄的原因；在监督微调、RL和预训练任务上改善了Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: Ember为语言模型嵌入层优化提供了内存高效的解决方案，证明了嵌入接口具有独特的几何特性；为Transformer训练优化器的选择提供了原则性解释；开源实现便于社区进一步研究，可与现有分布式训练框架无缝集成。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [136] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedCGNM优化器，通过分组归一化动量解决联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题，并引入FedHOO算法优化采样率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题导致少数类预测性能差，而隐私和异构性约束使得传统的中心化方法无法直接应用。

Method: 提出FedCGNM客户端优化器：将类别按组内最小方差分组，为每组维护动量并归一化为单位长度，使用归一化动量之和作为更新方向；引入基于X-armed-bandit的FedHOO算法，利用联邦并行性以线性成本优化重采样率。

Result: 在四个公开长尾基准数据集和一个专有芯片缺陷数据集上，FedCGNM持续优于基线方法，FedHOO在小规模联邦场景下进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: FedCGNM有效平衡了多数类和少数类之间的梯度幅度，减轻了稀有类梯度中的噪声影响，为联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction. This design both equalizes gradient magnitude across majority and minority groups and mitigates the noise inherent in rare-class gradients. We further provide a theoretical convergence analysis explicitly accounting for time-varying resampling-rates. Additionally, to efficiently optimize these rates in small-client regimes, we introduce FedHOO, an X-armed-bandit (XAB) based algorithm that exploits federated parallelism that evaluates many combinations of two candidate rates per client at linear cost. Empirical evaluation on four public long-tailed benchmarks and a proprietary chip-defect dataset demonstrates that FedCGNM consistently outperforms baselines, with FedHOO yielding further gains in small-scale federations.

</details>


### [137] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个三阶段扩展定律，将训练数据分解为训练步数和批量大小，从而能够更高效地拟合扩展律并恢复最优批量大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩展定律主要关注模型大小和训练数据量，但没有显式区分训练步数和批量大小。研究者希望提出一个更细粒度的扩展定律，能够更好地理解批量大小对模型训练的影响，并用更少的训练实验来拟合扩展律。

Method: 提出了三阶段扩展定律(three-term law)，将训练数据显式分解为训练步数和批量大小两个独立项。通过在大量训练实验上进行拟合，验证该定律的有效性。该方法能够利用非最优批量大小的训练运行来拟合扩展律。
